	public class giftbag
	{
		public int id;

		public int cost;

		public int required_level;

		public int required_vip_level;

		public giftbag(int _id, int _cost, int _required_level, int _required_vip_level)
		{
			id = _id;
			cost = _cost;
			required_level = _required_level;
			required_vip_level = _required_vip_level;
		}

		public giftbag()
		{
		}
	}

	public class giftbagitem
	{
		public int id;

		public int idgift_bag;

		public int treasure_type;

		public int param0;

		public int param1;

		public giftbagitem(int _id, int _idgift_bag, int _treasure_type, int _param0, int _param1)
		{
			id = _id;
			idgift_bag = _idgift_bag;
			treasure_type = _treasure_type;
			param0 = _param0;
			param1 = _param1;
		}

		public giftbagitem()
		{
		}
	}

	public void MyLoading(uint genID)
	{
		setmyloading();
		genid = genID;
		StartLoading();
	}

	public void setmyloading()
	{
		cs_premuim_get_gold();
		BaseAsset.lds.Visible_sub();
		DisableAll();
		AddAsset("ui_premium");
		AddAsset("ui_spiriticon");
		AddAsset("ui_main");
		CollectingStart(bCollecting: false);
		_treasure_type treasureTypeTemplate = Global.getTreasureTypeTemplate(9u);
		AddAsset(treasureTypeTemplate.name);
		treasureTypeTemplate = Global.getTreasureTypeTemplate(10u);
		AddAsset(treasureTypeTemplate.name);
		treasureTypeTemplate = Global.getTreasureTypeTemplate(30u);
		AddAsset(treasureTypeTemplate.name);
		initPremiumPages();
		for (int i = 0; i < 54; i++)
		{
			if ((checkDay[pageidx[i]] != null && checkDay[pageidx[i]]()) || debugFlag)
			{
				AddAsset("premium/ui_di" + (i + 1));
			}
		}
		if (!checkMingyue() && !debugFlag)
		{
			return;
		}
		friendShip = 40;
		for (int j = 1; j <= Global.character_.quest22; j++)
		{
			if (Global.getTemplate("premium_quest", (uint)j) is _premium_quest premium_quest)
			{
				friendShip += premium_quest.friendship;
			}
		}
		curBarWidth = (newBarWidth = (float)friendShip / 100f * 231f);
	}

	public void MyLoading()
	{
		uint genID = 0u;
		MyLoading(genID);
	}

	public void MyLoading(int targetid)
	{
		setmyloading();
		targetpage = targetid;
		StartLoading();
	}

	public override void Start()
	{
		scroll_index_chongzhi = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_zijiang = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_tiaozhan = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_Onetarget = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_Twotarget = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_Threetarget = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_Fourtarget = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_Fivetarget = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_Sixtarget = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_SevenTarget = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_RechargeActivity = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_caishiruku = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_zhaocaijinbao = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_everydaylogin = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_exchangeshop = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		base.Start();
		scroll_index_ChongJi = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_fund[0] = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		scroll_index_fund[1] = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
		{
			scroll_index_activity_quest[i] = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		}
		for (int j = 0; j < 5; j++)
		{
			costtimes_scrollid[j] = ScrollControl.RegisterInfo();
		}
	}

	private void Update()
	{
		if (curBarWidth < newBarWidth)
		{
			curBarWidth += 40f * Time.deltaTime;
			if (curBarWidth > newBarWidth)
			{
				curBarWidth = newBarWidth;
			}
		}
		if (curBarWidth > newBarWidth)
		{
			curBarWidth = newBarWidth;
		}
		if (light_state == 0)
		{
			light_alpha -= Time.deltaTime;
			if ((double)light_alpha < 0.1)
			{
				light_alpha = 0.1f;
				light_state = 1;
			}
		}
		else if (light_state == 1)
		{
			light_alpha += Time.deltaTime;
			if (light_alpha > 1f)
			{
				light_alpha = 1f;
				light_state = 0;
			}
		}
		UpdateOT();
		updatelotty();
	}

	protected override void Show()
	{
		BroadcastMessage("DisableMouseDown", SendMessageOptions.DontRequireReceiver);
		base.Show();
		rmg.ToEnable();
		GUI.enabled = true;
		BaseAsset.lds.Hide();
		MainUI.premuimEnter = true;
		m_LoadOverTime = DateTime.Now.AddSeconds(0.2);
		AssetBundle asset = BaseAsset.was.GetAsset("ui_premium");
		AssetBundle asset2 = BaseAsset.was.GetAsset("ui_spiriticon");
		AssetBundle asset3 = BaseAsset.was.GetAsset("ui_main");
		ui_bar.make(asset, "ui_bar");
		ui_bar_down.normal.background = asset.Load("ui_bar_down") as Texture2D;
		for (int i = 0; i < 54; i++)
		{
			if ((checkDay[pageidx[i]] != null && checkDay[pageidx[i]]()) || debugFlag)
			{
				AssetBundle asset4 = BaseAsset.was.GetAsset("premium/ui_di" + (i + 1));
				if (asset4 != null)
				{
					ui_di[pageidx[i]] = new GUIStyle();
					ui_di[pageidx[i]].normal.background = asset4.Load("ui_di" + (i + 1)) as Texture2D;
				}
				ui_zi[pageidx[i]] = new GUIStyle();

			}

			ui_zijiang_zi[j].normal.background = asset.Load("ui_zijiang_zi" + (j + 1)) as Texture2D;

		ui_gou.normal.background = asset.Load("ui_gou") as Texture2D;

		ui_baoxiang3.normal.background = asset.Load("ui_baoxiang3") as Texture2D;
		for (int k = 0; k < 9; k++)
		{
			ui_long[k] = new GUIStyle();
			ui_long[k].normal.background = asset.Load("ui_long" + (k + 1)) as Texture2D;
			ui_jiangli[k] = new GUIStyle();
			ui_jiangli[k].normal.background = asset.Load("ui_shenlong_baoxiang" + (k + 1)) as Texture2D;
		}
		ui_qianwang2.make(asset, "ui_qianwang2");
		ui_lingqu_light.normal.background = asset.Load("ui_lingqu_light") as Texture2D;
		ui_fudai.make(asset, "ui_fudai");
		ui_fudai.btnStyle.overflow = new RectOffset(0, 0, 13, 13);
		ui_fudai_di.normal.background = asset.Load("ui_fudai_di") as Texture2D;
		ui_yiling.normal.background = asset.Load("ui_yiling") as Texture2D;
		ui_qianwang.make(asset, "ui_qianwang");
		pageList.Clear();
		sended = false;
		for (int l = 0; l < 54; l++)
		{
			if ((checkDay[l] != null && checkDay[l]()) || debugFlag)
			{
				pageList.Add(l);
				if ((l == pageidx[12] && curPage != pageList.Count - 1) || !checkMingyue())
				{
					curPage = pageList.Count - 1;
				}
			}
		}
		if (pageList.Count > 0)
		{
			curPage = 0;
		}
		pagenum = 0;
		switch (genid)
		{
		case 211u:
		{
			if (!checkMingyue())
			{
				break;
			}
			for (int num2 = 0; num2 < pageList.Count; num2++)
			{
				if (pageList[num2] == pageidx[12])
				{
					curPage = num2;
					pagenum = num2 / 7;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 234u:
			if (checkZhaoZiLong())
			{
				for (int n = 0; n < pageList.Count; n++)
				{
					if (pageList[n] == pageidx[14])
					{
						curPage = n;
						pagenum = n / 7;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (!checkZhaoZiLongNew())
				{
					break;
				}
				for (int num = 0; num < pageList.Count; num++)
				{
					if (pageList[num] == pageidx[35])
					{
						curPage = num;
						pagenum = num / 7;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			break;
		case 282u:
		{
			if (!checkXinChunTeHui())
			{
				break;
			}
			for (int m = 0; m < pageList.Count; m++)
			{
				if (pageList[m] == pageidx[31])
				{
					curPage = m;
					pagenum = m / 7;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		}
		if (targetpage != -1)
		{
			for (int num3 = 0; num3 < pageList.Count; num3++)
			{
				if (pageList[num3] == pageidx[targetpage])
				{
					curPage = num3;
					pagenum = num3 / 7;
					break;
				}
			}
			targetpage = -1;
		}
		ui_down.make(asset, "ui_down");
		ui_up.make(asset, "ui_up");
		ui_zi112.normal.background = asset.Load("ui_zi112") as Texture2D;
		ui_zi113.normal.background = asset.Load("ui_zi113") as Texture2D;
		for (int num4 = 1; num4 <= 3; num4++)
		{
			ui_di11_bars[num4 - 1] = new GUIStyle();
			ui_di11_bars[num4 - 1].normal.background = asset.Load("ui_di11_bar" + num4) as Texture2D;
		}
		ui_chongzhi3.make(asset, "ui_chongzhi3");
		ui_jihuoma.make(asset, "ui_jihuoma");
		List<FestivalConsume> list = new List<FestivalConsume>();
		m_fclist.Clear();
		foreach (FestivalConsume value in Global.character_.festivalconsumes.Values)
		{
			list.Add(value);
		}
		while (list.Count > 0)
		{
			FestivalConsume festivalConsume = null;
			foreach (FestivalConsume item in list)
			{
				if (festivalConsume == null)
				{
					festivalConsume = item;
				}
				else if (festivalConsume.day > item.day)
				{
					festivalConsume = item;
				}
			}
			m_fclist.Add(festivalConsume);
			list.Remove(festivalConsume);
		}
		ui_hongbao1.normal.background = asset.Load("ui_hongbao1") as Texture2D;
		ui_hongbao2.normal.background = asset.Load("ui_hongbao2") as Texture2D;
		ui_lingqu3.make(asset, "ui_lingqu3");
		__BaseUI.init_lable(asset, "ui_xin1", ref ui_xin1);
		__BaseUI.init_lable(asset, "ui_xin2", ref ui_xin2);
		__BaseUI.init_lable(asset, "ui_zi_hgd", ref ui_zi_hgd);
		__BaseUI.init_lable(asset, "ui_haogan", ref ui_haogan);
		ui_haogan.border = new RectOffset(50, 50, 0, 0);
		__BaseUI.init_lable(asset, "ui_zi_ysj", ref ui_zi_ysj);
		__BaseUI.init_lable(asset, "ui_haoganbar1", ref ui_haoganbar1);
		__BaseUI.init_lable(asset, "ui_haoganbar2", ref ui_haoganbar2);
		__BaseUI.init_lable(asset, "ui_haoganbar3", ref ui_haoganbar3);
		__BaseUI.init_lable(asset, "ui_zi_gmyf", ref ui_zi_quest[0]);

	private int getpageid(int pageid)
	{
		foreach (KeyValuePair<int, int> item in pageidx)
		{
			if (item.Value == pageList[pageid])
			{
				return item.Key;
			}
		}
		return -1;
	}

	public override void LoadSuccessSub(string AssetName)
	{
		base.LoadSuccessSub(AssetName);
	}

	protected override void Web_OnGUI()
	{
		BeginGUI(800f, 480f, __BaseUI.UIDepth_Sub);
		if (m_LoadOverTime > DateTime.Now)
		{
			double totalSeconds = (m_LoadOverTime - DateTime.Now).TotalSeconds;
			double num = (0.5 - totalSeconds) / 0.5;
			Color backgroundColor = GUI.backgroundColor;
			GUI.backgroundColor = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, (float)num);
			GUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(0f, 0f, w, h), string.Empty, __BaseUI._bgStyle);
			GUI.backgroundColor = backgroundColor;
		}
		else
		{
			GUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(0f, 0f, w, h), string.Empty, __BaseUI._bgStyle);
			GUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(10f, 9f, 150f, 41f), string.Empty, titleStyle);
			if (ui_di[pageList[curPage]] != null)
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(169f, 61f, 610f, 397f), string.Empty, ui_di[pageList[curPage]]);
			}
			if (drawPage[pageList[curPage]] != null)
			{
				drawPage[pageList[curPage]]();
			}
			int num2 = 0;
			pagemax = (pageList.Count - 1) / 7;
			GUI.BeginGroup(__BaseUI._rect(20f, 86f, 171f, 343f));
			int num3 = -1 * pagenum * 49 * 7;
			if (DownTimer > DateTime.Now)
			{
				double num4 = (1.0 - (DownTimer - DateTime.Now).TotalSeconds / 0.3) * 49.0 * 7.0;
				num3 -= (int)num4;
			}
			else if (UpTimer > DateTime.Now)
			{
				double num5 = (1.0 - (UpTimer - DateTime.Now).TotalSeconds / 0.3) * 49.0 * 7.0;
				num3 += (int)num5;
			}
			else if (downMoving)
			{
				downMoving = false;
				pagenum++;
				curPage = pagenum * 7;
			}
			else if (upMoving)
			{
				upMoving = false;
				pagenum--;
				curPage = pagenum * 7;
			}
			GUI.BeginGroup(__BaseUI._rect(0f, num3, 171f, 2646f));
			for (int i = 0; i < pageList.Count; i++)
			{
				if (i != curPage)
				{
					if (ui_bar.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(0f, 49 * num2, 171f, 50f), string.Empty))
					{
						if (pageList[i] == pageidx[36])
						{
							clicknum = i;
							cs_online_lottery_enter(needcurpage: true);
						}
						else
						{
							curPage = i;
							showResult = false;
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(0f, 49 * num2, 171f, 50f), string.Empty, ui_bar_down);
				}
				if (pageList[i] == pageidx[28] || pageList[i] == pageidx[29] || pageList[i] == pageidx[30])
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(15f, 14 + 49 * num2, 25f, 19f), string.Empty, ui_cz_jinbi);
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(38f, 14 + 49 * num2, 92f, 20f), string.Empty, ui_zi[pageList[i]]);
				}
				else
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(15f, 14 + 49 * num2, 92f, 20f), string.Empty, ui_zi[pageList[i]]);
				}
				if (checkAward[pageList[i]] != null && checkAward[pageList[i]]())
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(119f, 0 + 49 * num2, 28f, 28f), string.Empty, __BaseUI._ui_jiang);
				}
				num2++;
			}
			GUI.EndGroup();
			GUI.EndGroup();
			if (DownTimer < DateTime.Now && UpTimer < DateTime.Now)
			{
				if (pagenum < pagemax && ui_down.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(61f, 428f, 56f, 38f), string.Empty))
				{
					DownTimer = DateTime.Now.AddSeconds(0.3);
					downMoving = true;
				}
				if (pagenum > 0 && ui_up.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(61f, 51f, 56f, 38f), string.Empty))
				{
					UpTimer = DateTime.Now.AddSeconds(0.3);
					upMoving = true;
				}
			}
		}
		if (EndGUI(hasClose: true, hasBlock: true, isX: false, hasFrameAnimation: false))
		{
			Hide();
			MainUI.SetSubUI(null);
		}
	}

	public void cs_online_lottery_enter(bool needcurpage)
	{
		DisableAll();
		CSOnlineLotteryEnter_0x745 pkt = new CSOnlineLotteryEnter_0x745();
		Global.m_client.Send(1861, pkt, sc_online_lottery_enter, needcurpage);
	}

	public void sc_online_lottery_enter(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			EnableAll();
			return;
		}
		SCOnlineLotteryEnter_0x745 sCOnlineLotteryEnter_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCOnlineLotteryEnter_0x745>(stream);
		if (sCOnlineLotteryEnter_0x.operate == SCOnlineLotteryEnter_0x745.Operate.OK)
		{
			Global.character_.onlinelottery.m_onlineLotteryItems.Clear();
			foreach (db_character_online_lottery_item lotteryitem in sCOnlineLotteryEnter_0x.lotteryitems)
			{
				OnlineLotteryItem onlineLotteryItem = new OnlineLotteryItem();
				onlineLotteryItem.Set(lotteryitem);
				Global.character_.onlinelottery.m_onlineLotteryItems.Add(onlineLotteryItem.iditem, onlineLotteryItem);
			}
			if ((bool)arg)
			{
				curPage = clicknum;
			}
			showResult = false;
		}
		else if (sCOnlineLotteryEnter_0x.operate != SCOnlineLotteryEnter_0x745.Operate.Error)
		{
		}
		EnableAll();
	}

	private int CheckDayAward()
	{
		uint register_time = Global.character_.register_time;
		float num = (float)(MainUI.get_Last_RefreshTime(TimeUtil.Now) - MainUI.get_Last_RefreshTime(register_time)) / 60f / 60f / 24f;
		return (int)num;
	}

	private bool CheckTodayAward()
	{
		return Global.character_.newbieinfo.day_award_time >= MainUI.get_Last_RefreshTime(TimeUtil.Now);
	}

	private void closeBonus()
	{
		EnableAll();
	}

	public override void drawTooltips(string tip)
	{

	protected override void AssetLoaded(string AssetName)
	{
		if (m_treasure != null && m_treasure.getIconName() == AssetName)
		{
			m_treasure.setTexIcon(BaseAsset.LoadTexture(AssetName));
		}
		else if (AssetName.Contains("premium/ui_di"))
		{
			Logger.Log(AssetName);
			int num = int.Parse(AssetName.Replace("premium/ui_di", string.Empty));
			if (ui_di[num - 1] == null)
			{
				ui_di[num - 1] = new GUIStyle();
			}
			ui_di[num - 1].normal.background = BaseAsset.was.GetAsset(AssetName).Load("ui_di" + num) as Texture2D;
		}
		if (AssetName.Contains("face"))
		{
			int num2 = int.Parse(AssetName.Replace("face", string.Empty));
			if (!purpleGenSty.ContainsKey(num2))
			{
				GUIStyle gUIStyle = new GUIStyle();
				gUIStyle.normal.background = BaseAsset.LoadTexture(AssetName);
				purpleGenSty.Add(num2, gUIStyle);
			}
			foreach (uint value in m_faceiddic.Values)
			{
				if (value == num2)
				{
					m_facedic[(uint)num2] = BaseAsset.LoadTexture(AssetName);
				}
			}
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < m_GiftBagTemp.Count; i++)
		{
			m_GiftBagTemp[i].setTexIcon(BaseAsset.LoadTexture(m_GiftBagTemp[i].getIconName()));
		}
		for (int j = 0; j < m_everydayloginTemp.Count; j++)
		{
			m_everydayloginTemp[j].setTexIcon(BaseAsset.LoadTexture(m_everydayloginTemp[j].getIconName()));
		}
		foreach (Database.CostTimesTemplate template in Global.costtimeswave.templates)
		{
			foreach (_Treasure bonu in template.bonus)
			{
				if (bonu.getIconName() == AssetName)
				{
					bonu.setTexIcon(BaseAsset.LoadTexture(AssetName));
				}
			}
		}
		if (m_t != null)
		{
			m_t.setTexIcon(BaseAsset.LoadTexture(m_t.getIconName()));
		}
		if (M_tre != null)
		{
			M_tre.setTexIcon(BaseAsset.LoadTexture(M_tre.getIconName()));
		}
		if (gb_sptre != null)
		{
			gb_sptre.setTexIcon(BaseAsset.LoadTexture(gb_sptre.getIconName()));
		}
		if (ExchangeTre != null)
		{
			ExchangeTre.setTexIcon(BaseAsset.LoadTexture(ExchangeTre.getIconName()));
		}
	}

	public void cs_premuim_get_gold()
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSPremuimGetGold_0x352 pkt = new CSPremuimGetGold_0x352();
			RootUI.m_client.Send(850, pkt, sc_premuim_get_gold);
		}
	}

	public void sc_premuim_get_gold(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream != null)
		{
			sended = false;
			SCPremuimGetGold_0x352 sCPremuimGetGold_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCPremuimGetGold_0x352>(stream);
			Global.character_.gold_consume = sCPremuimGetGold_0x.gold;
		}
	}

	private bool checkVipLevelRewards(int level)
	{
		if (level > Global.character_.VIPLEVEL)
		{
			return false;
		}
		foreach (_vip_rewards value in Global.getTemplates("vip_rewards").Values)
		{
			if (value.vip_level == level && !Global.character_.viprewards.Contains(value.id))
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	private void ResumeUI()
	{
		EnableAll();
	}

	public bool CheckCaiShiRuKu()
	{
		return Global.isNewNewbieActivity && MainUI.CalcServerOpenRefreshDaysTest() < 3;
	}

	public bool CheckZhaoCaiJinBao()
	{
		return Global.isNewNewbieActivity && MainUI.CalcServerOpenRefreshDaysTest() < 3;
	}

	public bool CheckZhaoXianNaShi()
	{
		return Global.isNewNewbieActivity && MainUI.CalcServerOpenRefreshDaysTest() < 3;
	}

	public bool CheckCaiShiRuKuAward()
	{
		Hashtable templates = Global.getTemplates("enchant_dig_award");
		for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
		{
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			if (Global.character_.newbieenchantrecords.ContainsKey(i))
			{
				num = Global.character_.newbieenchantrecords[i].dig_times;
				num2 = Global.character_.newbieenchantrecords[i].gold_dig_times;
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < templates.Count; j++)
			{
				_enchant_dig_award enchant_dig_award = Global.getTemplate("enchant_dig_award", (uint)(j + 1)) as _enchant_dig_award;
				if (Global.character_.getEnchantAward(i, enchant_dig_award.id) != null)
				{
					if (Global.character_.getEnchantAward(i, enchant_dig_award.id).got)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (enchant_dig_award.isgold)
					{
						if (num2 >= enchant_dig_award.times)
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
					else if (num >= enchant_dig_award.times)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				else if (enchant_dig_award.isgold)
				{
					if (num2 >= enchant_dig_award.times)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				else if (num >= enchant_dig_award.times)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public bool CheckZhaoCaiJinBaoAward()
	{
		Hashtable templates = Global.getTemplates("newbie_exchange_award");
		for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
		{
			int num = 0;
			if (Global.character_.newbieenchantrecords.ContainsKey(i))
			{
				num = Global.character_.newbieenchantrecords[i].exchange_times;
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < templates.Count; j++)
			{
				_newbie_exchange_award newbie_exchange_award = Global.getTemplate("newbie_exchange_award", (uint)(j + 1)) as _newbie_exchange_award;
				if (Global.character_.getExchangeAward(i, newbie_exchange_award.id) != null)
				{
					if (!Global.character_.getExchangeAward(i, newbie_exchange_award.id).got && num >= newbie_exchange_award.times)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				else if (num >= newbie_exchange_award.times)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public bool CheckZhaoXianNaShiAward()
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
		{
			_newbie_general_award newbie_general_award = Global.getTemplate("newbie_general_award", (uint)(i + 1)) as _newbie_general_award;
			for (int j = 0; j < 3; j++)
			{
				int num = DrawThreeTime(__BaseUI._rect(0f, 0f, 0f, 0f), j, draw: false);
				if (num == 1 && (Global.character_.getGeneralAward(j, newbie_general_award.id) == null || !Global.character_.getGeneralAward(j, newbie_general_award.id).got) && !check_recruit_gen((ushort)newbie_general_award.genid))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public void DrawTime(Rect rt)
	{
		float num = 0f;
		float num2 = 0f;
		num = Global.serverOpenTimeTest;
		num2 = Global.serverOpenTimeTest + 172800;
		DateTime dateTime = new DateTime(1970, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc).AddSeconds(num).ToLocalTime();
		DateTime dateTime2 = new DateTime(1970, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc).AddSeconds(num2).ToLocalTime();
		__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(rt, __BaseUI.load_str(21437u, dateTime.Month, dateTime.Day, dateTime2.Month, dateTime2.Day), __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite);
	}

	public int DrawThreeTime(Rect rt, int day, bool draw)
	{
		int num = 0;
		int num2 = 0;
		num = (int)MainUI.New_get_Last_RefreshTime((uint)(Global.serverOpenTimeTest + day * 60 * 60 * 24));
		num2 = (int)MainUI.New_get_Last_RefreshTime((uint)(Global.serverOpenTimeTest + (day + 1) * 60 * 60 * 24));
		DateTime dateTime = new DateTime(1970, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc).AddSeconds(num).ToLocalTime();
		DateTime dateTime2 = new DateTime(1970, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc).AddSeconds(num2).ToLocalTime();
		if (draw)
		{
			__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(rt, "(" + dateTime.Month + "/" + dateTime.Day + " " + dateTime.Hour + ":00-" + dateTime2.Month + "/" + dateTime2.Day + " " + dateTime2.Hour + ":00)", __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrGreen);
		}
		if (TimeUtil.Now >= num)
		{
			if (TimeUtil.Now < num2)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			return 2;
		}
		return 0;
	}

	public void DrawCaiShiRuKu()
	{
		DrawTime(__BaseUI._rect(285f, 425f, 300f, 16f));
		Hashtable templates = Global.getTemplates("enchant_dig_award");

				__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(190f, 6f, 23f, 23f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "yindao:" + enchant_dig_award.treasure_type + "|" + enchant_dig_award.param0 + "|" + enchant_dig_award.param1), GetIcon(enchant_dig_award.treasure_type));

			}

		}
		GUIHacks.EndScrollView();
	}

	private bool check_recruit_gen(ushort id)
	{
		_general_origin general_origin = Global.getTemplate("general_origin", id) as _general_origin;
		List<ushort> list = new List<ushort>();
		if (general_origin != null)
		{
			Hashtable templates = Global.getTemplates("general_origin");
			foreach (DictionaryEntry item in templates)
			{
				_general_origin general_origin2 = item.Value as _general_origin;
				if (general_origin2.origin == general_origin.origin && general_origin2.form > general_origin.form)
				{
					list.Add(general_origin2.idgeneral_template);
				}
			}
		}
		list.Add(id);
		foreach (KeyValuePair<ushort, FormGeneral> item2 in Global.character_.form_generals_)
		{
			if (list.Contains(item2.Value.idgeneral_template))
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		foreach (GeneralsSimple item3 in Global.character_.GeneralsOutSquad)
		{
			if (list.Contains((ushort)item3.idtemplate))
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		return true;
	}

	private Texture2D GetIcon(int treid, int param0, int param1)
	{
		Texture2D result = null;
		switch (treid)
		{
		case 4:
			result = __BaseUI._iconMoney;
			break;
		case 5:
			result = __BaseUI._iconMana;
			break;
		case 6:
			result = __BaseUI._iconPractice;
			break;
		case 8:
			result = __BaseUI._tex2Dexp;
			break;
		case 10:
			result = __BaseUI._iconEnchant;
			break;
		case 9:
			result = __BaseUI._iconFruit;
			break;
		case 51:
			result = __BaseUI._icondragonball4;
			break;
		case 55:
			result = __BaseUI._iconjinengsuipian;
			break;
		case 46:
			result = __BaseUI._iconGemEssence;
			break;
		case 18:
			result = __BaseUI._iconReputation;
			break;
		case 39:
			result = __BaseUI.icon_purplesoul;
			break;
		case 40:
			result = __BaseUI.icon_orangesoul;
			break;
		case 30:
			result = __BaseUI._iconStone;
			break;
		case 45:
			result = __BaseUI._iconGemShard;
			break;
		case 38:
			result = __BaseUI.icon_bluesoul;
			break;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public void cs_newbie_enchant_award_get(uint day, uint id)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSNewbieEnchantAwardGet_0x3B8 cSNewbieEnchantAwardGet_0x3B = new CSNewbieEnchantAwardGet_0x3B8();
			cSNewbieEnchantAwardGet_0x3B.id = id;
			cSNewbieEnchantAwardGet_0x3B.day = day;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(952, cSNewbieEnchantAwardGet_0x3B, sc_newbie_enchant_award_get);
		}
	}

	public void sc_newbie_enchant_award_get(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCNewbieEnchantAwardGet_0x3B8 sCNewbieEnchantAwardGet_0x3B = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCNewbieEnchantAwardGet_0x3B8>(stream);
		if (sCNewbieEnchantAwardGet_0x3B.result != 1)
		{
			return;
		}
		List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
		_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
		treasure.make(sCNewbieEnchantAwardGet_0x3B.bonus);
		list.Add(treasure);
		if (list.Count > 0)
		{
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, EnableAll);
			battleResultUI.PrepareLoading("nocollecting");
			battleResultUI.StartLoading();
		}
		if (sCNewbieEnchantAwardGet_0x3B.newbieenchantaward != null)
		{
			NewbieEnchantAward newbieEnchantAward = new NewbieEnchantAward();
			newbieEnchantAward.Set(sCNewbieEnchantAwardGet_0x3B.newbieenchantaward);
			if (Global.character_.newbieenchantawards.ContainsKey(sCNewbieEnchantAwardGet_0x3B.newbieenchantaward.id))
			{
				Global.character_.newbieenchantawards[sCNewbieEnchantAwardGet_0x3B.newbieenchantaward.id] = newbieEnchantAward;
			}
			else
			{
				Global.character_.newbieenchantawards.Add(newbieEnchantAward.id, newbieEnchantAward);
			}
		}
	}

	}

		{
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, EnableAll);
			battleResultUI.PrepareLoading("nocollecting");
			battleResultUI.StartLoading();
		}
		if (sCNewbieGeneralAwardGet_0x3BB.newbiegeneralaward != null)
		{
			NewbieGeneralAward newbieGeneralAward = new NewbieGeneralAward();
			newbieGeneralAward.Set(sCNewbieGeneralAwardGet_0x3BB.newbiegeneralaward);
			if (Global.character_.newbiegeneralawards.ContainsKey(sCNewbieGeneralAwardGet_0x3BB.newbiegeneralaward.id))
			{
				Global.character_.newbiegeneralawards[sCNewbieGeneralAwardGet_0x3BB.newbiegeneralaward.id] = newbieGeneralAward;
			}
			else
			{
				Global.character_.newbiegeneralawards.Add(newbieGeneralAward.id, newbieGeneralAward);
			}
		}
	}

	public bool goAcitivityQuest(uint questid)
	{
		bool result = false;
		switch (questid)
		{
		case 2u:
		case 9u:
			if (MainUI.checkUI("Capital"))
			{
				MainUI.SetSubUI(null);
				MainUI._capital();
				result = true;
			}
			break;
		case 6u:
			if (MainUI._huodong_check("huodong"))
			{
				MainUI._huodong();
				result = true;
			}
			break;
		case 1u:
		case 5u:
		case 7u:
		case 13u:
			if (MainUI.checkUI("social_friend"))
			{
				MainUI._social_friend();
				result = true;
			}
			break;
		case 4u:
		case 8u:
		case 14u:
			if (MainUI.checkUI("social_guild"))
			{
				MainUI._social_guild();
				result = true;
			}
			break;
		case 3u:
			if (MainUI.checkStarTechUI("army_star"))
			{
				MainUI._army_star();
				result = true;
			}
			break;
		case 10u:
			if (MainUI._huodong_check("huodong") && Global.character_.idguild != 0L)
			{
				SealDevilGroupUI component = BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SealDevilGroupUI>();
				if (component != null)
				{
					MainUI.SetSubUI(component);
					component.SubLoading();
				}
				result = true;
			}
			break;
		case 11u:
			if (MainUI.checkUI("general_person"))
			{
				MainUI._general_person();
				result = true;
			}
			break;
		case 12u:
			if (MainUI.checkUI("army_recruit"))
			{
				MainUI._general_book();
				result = true;
			}
			break;
		case 15u:
			if (MainUI.checkUI("grave"))
			{
				MainUI._grave();
				result = true;
			}
			break;
		case 16u:
			if (MainUI.checkUI("wood_war"))
			{
				MainUI._muren();
				result = true;
			}
			break;
		case 17u:
			if (MainUI.checkUI("anecdote"))
			{
				MainUI._anecdote();
				result = true;
			}
			break;
		case 18u:
			if (MainUI.checkUI("trial_maze"))
			{
				MainUI._trial_maze();
				result = true;
			}
			break;
		}
		return result;
	}

	{
		Rect rc = new Rect(511f, 68f, 259f, 383f);

		{
			seeGenDetail(234u);
		}
		if (Global.character_.VIPLEVEL < 7 && Global.character_.quest69 < 4 && chargeBtn.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(257f, 218f, 217f, 49f), string.Empty))
		{
			RootUI.Coin();
		}
		if (!MainUI.checkUI("army_recruit"))
		{
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(214f, 283f, 291f, 50f), string.Empty, ui_bar_4);
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(258f, 300f, 182f, 15f), string.Empty, ui_zi_zm);
		}
		else
		{
			if (!(Global.getTemplate("premium_general_quest", 69u) is _premium_general_quest premium_general_quest))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (Global.character_.quest69 == 4 || Global.character_.VIPLEVEL >= 7)
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(233f, 249f, 275f, 88f), string.Empty, ui_bar_5);
				if (recruitBtn.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(357f, 297f, 98f, 30f), string.Empty))
				{
					cs_zhaoyun_quest_new(Global.isNewNewbieActivity);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < questDescs.Length; i++)
				{
					if (!(Global.getTemplate("premium_quest", (uint)(i + premium_general_quest.quest_startid)) is _premium_quest))
					{
						return;
					}
					if (i == Global.character_.quest69)
					{
						__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(214f, 293 + step * i, 291f, 28f), string.Empty, ui_bar_3);
						__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(240f, 293 + step * i, 50f, 28f), __BaseUI.load_str(20854u) + (i + 1), __BaseUI._textLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite, __BaseUI._fontSize);
						if (questDescs[i] != null)
						{
							questDescs[i].draw(__BaseUI._rect(290f, 299 + step * i, 150f, 28f), __BaseUI._textNLeft);
						}
						if (Global.character_.quest69 == 0)
						{
							if (Global.character_.level >= 70)
							{
								if (ui_lingqu2.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
								{
									cs_zhaoyun_quest_new(Global.isNewNewbieActivity);
								}
							}
							else if (btnGoldPay.Button(Global.character_.VIPLEVEL >= 5, __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "goldzhaozilong")))
							{
								ZhaoyunGoldCostNewer();
							}
						}
						else if (Global.character_.quest69 == 1)
						{
							if (doHaveGeneral(69u))
							{
								if (ui_lingqu2.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
								{
									cs_zhaoyun_quest_new(Global.isNewNewbieActivity);
								}
							}
							else if (btnGoldPay.Button(Global.character_.VIPLEVEL >= 5, __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "goldzhaozilong")))
							{
								ZhaoyunGoldCostNewer();
							}
						}
						else
						{
							if (Global.character_.quest69 != 2 && Global.character_.quest69 != 3)
							{
								continue;
							}
							if (checkQuestNeed(Global.character_.quest69 + premium_general_quest.quest_startid))
							{
								if (btnPay.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
								{
									cs_zhaoyun_quest_new(Global.isNewNewbieActivity);
								}
							}
							else if (btnGoldPay.Button(Global.character_.VIPLEVEL >= 5, __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "goldzhaozilong")))
							{
								ZhaoyunGoldCostNewer();
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(240f, 293 + step * i, 50f, 28f), __BaseUI.load_str(20854u) + (i + 1), __BaseUI._textLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite, __BaseUI._fontSize);
						questDescs[i].draw(__BaseUI._rect(290f, 299 + step * i, 150f, 28f), __BaseUI._textNLeft);
						__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(214f, 293 + step * i, 291f, 28f), string.Empty, ui_bar_4);
					}
				}
			}
			if (Global.character_.VIPLEVEL >= 5)
			{
				int num = 0;
				if (TimeUtil.Now < Global.character_.vip5_start_time + 259200)
				{
					num = (int)(Global.character_.vip5_start_time + 259200 - TimeUtil.Now);
				}
				if (num != 0)
				{

				}
				else
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(520f, 425f, 223f, 24f), string.Empty, ui_vjieshu);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public void cs_activity_quest_award_get_0x722(uint questid)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSActivityQuestAwardGet_0x722 cSActivityQuestAwardGet_0x = new CSActivityQuestAwardGet_0x722();
			cSActivityQuestAwardGet_0x.questid = questid;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(1826, cSActivityQuestAwardGet_0x, sc_activity_quest_award_get_0x722);
		}
	}

	public void sc_activity_quest_award_get_0x722(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			sended = false;
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCActivityQuestAwardGet_0x722 sCActivityQuestAwardGet_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCActivityQuestAwardGet_0x722>(stream);
		if (sCActivityQuestAwardGet_0x.operate != SCActivityQuestAwardGet_0x722.Operate.OK)
		{
			return;
		}
		Global.character_.activity_quest_mgr.m_activityquests.Clear();
		foreach (db_character_activity_quest activityquest in sCActivityQuestAwardGet_0x.activityquests)
		{
			ActivityQuest activityQuest = new ActivityQuest();
			activityQuest.Set(activityquest);
			Global.character_.activity_quest_mgr.m_activityquests.Add(activityQuest.idquest, activityQuest);
		}
		List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
		foreach (Treasure bonu in sCActivityQuestAwardGet_0x.bonus)
		{
			_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
			treasure.make(bonu);
			list.Add(treasure);
		}
		BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
		battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, ResumeUI);
		battleResultUI.MyLoading();
	}

	private void ZhaoyunGoldCostNewer()
	{
		if (TimeUtil.Now > Global.character_.vip5_start_time + 259200)
		{
			__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(9231u), rmg);
		}
		else if (Global.getTemplate("premium_general_quest", 69u) is _premium_general_quest premium_general_quest && Global.getTemplate("premium_quest_new", (uint)(Global.character_.quest69 + premium_general_quest.quest_startid)) is _premium_quest_new premium_quest_new)
		{
			__BaseUI.GetConsumeUI().setConsume(premium_quest_new.gold_cost, 54, ZhaoyunGoldOKNewer, new object[1] { Global.isNewerZhaoYunQuest });
		}
	}

	private void ZhaoyunGoldOKNewer(bool bOK, object[] arg)
	{
		if (bOK && arg != null)
		{
			bool isnewerzhaoyunquest = (bool)arg[0];
			cs_zhaoyun_quest_gold_newer(isnewerzhaoyunquest);
		}
	}

	public void cs_zhaoyun_quest_gold_newer(bool _isnewerzhaoyunquest)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSZhaoyunQuestGoldNewer_0x729 cSZhaoyunQuestGoldNewer_0x = new CSZhaoyunQuestGoldNewer_0x729();
			cSZhaoyunQuestGoldNewer_0x.isNewerZhaoYunQuest = _isnewerzhaoyunquest;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(1833, cSZhaoyunQuestGoldNewer_0x, sc_zhaoyun_quest_gold_newer);
		}
	}

	public void sc_zhaoyun_quest_gold_newer(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCZhaoyunQuestGoldNewer_0x729 sCZhaoyunQuestGoldNewer_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCZhaoyunQuestGoldNewer_0x729>(stream);
		switch (sCZhaoyunQuestGoldNewer_0x.operate)
		{
		case SCZhaoyunQuestGoldNewer_0x729.Operate.OK:
		{
			Global.character_.quest69 = (byte)sCZhaoyunQuestGoldNewer_0x.step;
			if (Global.getTemplate("premium_general_quest", 69u) is _premium_general_quest premium_general_quest && Global.getTemplate("premium_quest_new", (uint)(Global.character_.quest69 - 1 + premium_general_quest.quest_startid)) is _premium_quest_new premium_quest_new)
			{
				Global.character_.gold -= premium_quest_new.gold_cost;
				__BaseUI.drawStateBubble("gold-" + premium_quest_new.gold_cost);
			}
			List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
			foreach (Treasure bonuse in sCZhaoyunQuestGoldNewer_0x.bonuses)
			{
				if (bonuse.idtreasure_type == 0 || bonuse.idtreasure_type == 36)
				{
					_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
					treasure.make(bonuse);
					BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpecialGetUI>().ShowSPGet(treasure);
				}
				else
				{
					_Treasure treasure2 = new _Treasure();
					treasure2.make(bonuse);
					list.Add(treasure2);
				}
			}
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, EnableAll);
			battleResultUI.MyLoading();
			break;
		}
		}
	}

	public bool CheckExchangeShop()
	{
		if (Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp.Count > 0)
		{
			uint start_time = Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[0].start_time;
			uint end_time = Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[0].end_time;
			if (start_time <= TimeUtil.Now && end_time >= TimeUtil.Now)
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public bool CheckEverydayLogin()
	{
		if (Global.character_.everydaylogintemp.Count > 0)
		{
			uint start_time = Global.character_.everydaylogintemp[0].start_time;
			uint end_time = Global.character_.everydaylogintemp[0].end_time;
			if (start_time <= TimeUtil.Now && end_time >= TimeUtil.Now)
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public bool CheckExchangeShopAward()
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp.Count; i++)
		{
			bool flag = true;
			for (int j = 0; j < Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[i].treasure_f.Count; j++)
			{
				if (!Global.character_.checkBonus(Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[i].treasure_f[j].type, Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[i].treasure_f[j].param[0], Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[i].treasure_f[j].param[1]))
				{
					flag = false;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (flag && !Global.character_.exchangeshops.ContainsKey(Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[i].id))
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public bool CheckEverydayLoginAward()
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < Global.character_.everydaylogintemp.Count; i++)
		{
			if (!Global.character_.everydaylogins.ContainsKey(Global.character_.everydaylogintemp[i].dayid) && Global.character_.everyday_login_days >= Global.character_.everydaylogintemp[i].dayid)
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public void DrawExchangeShop()
	{
		int num = 83;

		ScrollControl.infos[scroll_index_exchangeshop].scrollpos = scrollPos_exchangeshop;
		for (int i = 0; i < Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp.Count; i++)

				if (ExchangeTre.texIcon != null)
				{
					if (ExchangeTre.texIcon.name != ExchangeTre.getIconName())
					{
						AddAssetSub(ExchangeTre.getIconName());
						StartLoadingSub();
					}
					if (ExchangeTre.texIcon.name == ExchangeTre.getIconName())
					{
						__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(25 + 62 * j, 11f, 42f, 42f), ui_gezi2);
						__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(29 + 62 * j, 15f, 34f, 34f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "exshop" + ExchangeTre.type + "|" + ExchangeTre.param[0] + "|" + ExchangeTre.param[1]), ExchangeTre.texIcon);
						if (!Global.character_.checkBonus(ExchangeTre.type, ExchangeTre.param[0], ExchangeTre.param[1]))
						{
							__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(10 + 62 * j, 49f, 68f, 16f), Global.getFormatNumber((uint)Global.character_.getBonusByIdTreasure(ExchangeTre.type)) + "/" + Global.getFormatNumber((uint)ExchangeTre.param[1]), __BaseUI._numNCenter, __BaseUI._clrRed, 11);
							flag = false;
						}
						else
						{
							__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(10 + 62 * j, 49f, 68f, 16f), Global.getFormatNumber((uint)Global.character_.getBonusByIdTreasure(ExchangeTre.type)) + "/" + Global.getFormatNumber((uint)ExchangeTre.param[1]), __BaseUI._numNCenter, __BaseUI._clrGreen, 11);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					AddAssetSub(ExchangeTre.getIconName());
					StartLoadingSub();
				}
			}
			bool flag2 = false;
			bool flag3 = false;
			for (int k = 0; k < Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[i].treasure_r.Count; k++)
			{
				ExchangeTre.make(Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[i].treasure_r[k].type, Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[i].treasure_r[k].param[0], Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[i].treasure_r[k].param[1]);
				if (ExchangeTre.texIcon != null)
				{
					if (ExchangeTre.texIcon.name != ExchangeTre.getIconName())
					{
						AddAssetSub(ExchangeTre.getIconName());
						StartLoadingSub();
					}
					__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(259 + 70 * k, 7f, 64f, 64f), ui_gezi1);
					__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(266 + 70 * k, 14f, 50f, 50f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "exshop" + ExchangeTre.type + "|" + ExchangeTre.param[0] + "|" + ExchangeTre.param[1]), ExchangeTre.texIcon);
					int num3 = ExchangeTre.param[1];
					if (ExchangeTre.type == 54 || ExchangeTre.type == 42)
					{
						num3 = 1;
					}
					__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(257 + 70 * k, 52f, 64f, 16f), Global.getFormatNumber((uint)num3), __BaseUI._numNCenter, __BaseUI._clrGreen);
					if (Global.character_.exchangeshops.ContainsKey(Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[i].id))
					{
						__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(262 + 70 * k, 12f, 58f, 58f), ui_esmengban);
						__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(255 + 70 * k, 13f, 71f, 49f), ui_esling);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					AddAssetSub(ExchangeTre.getIconName());
					StartLoadingSub();
				}
				if ((Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[i].treasure_r[k].type == 54 || Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[i].treasure_r[k].type == 42) && Global.character_.isGemFull())
				{
					flag2 = true;

				if (Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[i].treasure_r[k].type == 36 && Global.character_.artifacts.ContainsKey((ushort)Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[i].treasure_r[k].param[0]))

				}
				else

					cs_exchange_shop_buy(Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[i].id);
				}
			}
			__BaseUI.EndGroup();
		}
		GUIHacks.EndScrollView();
		if (Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp.Count > 0)
		{
			uint start_time = Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[0].start_time;
			uint end_time = Global.character_.exchangeshoptemp[0].end_time;
			DateTime dateTime = new DateTime(1970, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc).AddSeconds(start_time).ToLocalTime();

			__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(429f, 126f, 300f, 16f), "(" + dateTime.Month + "/" + dateTime.Day + " " + dateTime.Hour + ":00-" + dateTime2.Month + "/" + dateTime2.Day + " " + dateTime2.Hour + ":00)", __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite);
		}
	}

	public void DrawEverydayLogin()
	{
		int num = 51;
		int num2 = Global.character_.everydaylogintemp.Count * num;
		scrollPos_everydaylogin = ScrollControl.infos[scroll_index_everydaylogin].scrollpos;
		scrollPos_everydaylogin = GUIHacks.BeginScrollView(__BaseUI._rect(230f, 155f, 468f, 255f), scrollPos_everydaylogin, __BaseUI._rect(0f, 0f, 468f, num2 + 3), useHorizontal: false, useVertical: true, new GUIStyle(), __BaseUI._vertScroll1[0], num, 0, scroll_index_everydaylogin);
		ScrollControl.infos[scroll_index_everydaylogin].scrollpos = scrollPos_everydaylogin;
		for (int i = 0; i < Global.character_.everydaylogintemp.Count; i++)

				__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(355f, 26f, 43f, 18f), ui_zi_yilingqu);
			}
			else

				{
					cs_everyday_login_get(Global.character_.everydaylogintemp[i].dayid);
				}
			}
			if (Global.character_.everydaylogintemp[i].dayid > 9)
			{
				uint num3 = Global.character_.everydaylogintemp[i].dayid / 10;
				uint num4 = Global.character_.everydaylogintemp[i].dayid - num3 * 10;
				__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(206f, 7f, 27f, 38f), ui_zi_num[num3]);
				__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(229f, 7f, 27f, 38f), ui_zi_num[num4]);

			else
			{
				__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(219f, 7f, 27f, 38f), ui_zi_num[Global.character_.everydaylogintemp[i].dayid]);
			}
			__BaseUI.EndGroup();
		}
		GUIHacks.EndScrollView();
		if (Global.character_.everydaylogintemp.Count > 0)
		{
			uint end_time = Global.character_.everydaylogintemp[0].end_time;
			DateTime dateTime = new DateTime(1970, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc).AddSeconds(end_time).ToLocalTime();
			__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(398f, 129f, 56f, 16f), dateTime.Month + "/" + dateTime.Day, __BaseUI._numNCenter, __BaseUI._clrWhite);
			int t = (int)(end_time - TimeUtil.Now);
			__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(550f, 129f, 300f, 16f), Global.getStringTime(t), __BaseUI._numNLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite);
		}
	}

	public void cs_everyday_login_get(uint id)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSEveryDayLoginGet_0x80F cSEveryDayLoginGet_0x80F = new CSEveryDayLoginGet_0x80F();
			cSEveryDayLoginGet_0x80F.dayid = id;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(2063, cSEveryDayLoginGet_0x80F, sc_everyday_login_get);
		}
	}

	private void sc_everyday_login_get(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCEveryDayLoginGet_0x80F sCEveryDayLoginGet_0x80F = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCEveryDayLoginGet_0x80F>(stream);
		if (sCEveryDayLoginGet_0x80F.result != 1)
		{
			return;
		}
		EveryDayLogin everyDayLogin = new EveryDayLogin();
		everyDayLogin.Set(sCEveryDayLoginGet_0x80F.dbc_everydaylogin);
		Global.character_.everydaylogins.Add(everyDayLogin.day, everyDayLogin);
		if (sCEveryDayLoginGet_0x80F.bonus != null)
		{
			List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
			for (int i = 0; i < sCEveryDayLoginGet_0x80F.bonus.Count; i++)
			{
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make((byte)sCEveryDayLoginGet_0x80F.bonus[i].idtreasure_type, sCEveryDayLoginGet_0x80F.bonus[i].param0, sCEveryDayLoginGet_0x80F.bonus[i].param1);
				list.Add(treasure);
			}
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, BonusClose);
			battleResultUI.MyLoading();
		}
	}

	public void cs_exchange_shop_buy(uint id)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSExchangeShopBuy_0x80E cSExchangeShopBuy_0x80E = new CSExchangeShopBuy_0x80E();
			cSExchangeShopBuy_0x80E.id = id;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(2062, cSExchangeShopBuy_0x80E, sc_exchange_shop_buy);
		}
	}

	private void sc_exchange_shop_buy(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCExchangeShopBuy_0x80E sCExchangeShopBuy_0x80E = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCExchangeShopBuy_0x80E>(stream);
		if (sCExchangeShopBuy_0x80E.result != 1)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (sCExchangeShopBuy_0x80E.cost != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < sCExchangeShopBuy_0x80E.cost.Count; i++)
			{
				Global.character_.remParam(sCExchangeShopBuy_0x80E.cost[i].idtreasure_type, sCExchangeShopBuy_0x80E.cost[i].param0, sCExchangeShopBuy_0x80E.cost[i].param1);
			}
		}
		Database.ExchangeShop exchangeShop = new Database.ExchangeShop();
		exchangeShop.Set(sCExchangeShopBuy_0x80E.dbc_exchangeshop);
		Global.character_.exchangeshops.Add(exchangeShop.idexchangeshop, exchangeShop);
		if (sCExchangeShopBuy_0x80E.bonus != null)
		{
			List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
			for (int j = 0; j < sCExchangeShopBuy_0x80E.bonus.Count; j++)
			{
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make((byte)sCExchangeShopBuy_0x80E.bonus[j].idtreasure_type, sCExchangeShopBuy_0x80E.bonus[j].param0, sCExchangeShopBuy_0x80E.bonus[j].param1);
				list.Add(treasure);
			}
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, BonusClose);
			battleResultUI.MyLoading();
		}
	}

		EnableAll();
	}

	public bool checkZijiangAward()
	{
		if (checkZijiang())
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (FormGeneral value in Global.character_.form_generals_.Values)
			{
				if (value.idgeneral_template != 0 && value.template.color > 3)
				{
					num++;
				}
			}
			foreach (GeneralsSimple item in Global.character_.GeneralsOutSquad)
			{
				if (item.template.color > 3)
				{
					num++;
				}
			}
			if (Global.character_.purple_gen_award < 3 && num > Global.character_.purple_gen_award)
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	}

			return true;

		{
			return false;
		}
		return false;
	}

	private void DrawJieri()
	{
		int num = 0;
		bool flag = false;
		int num2 = 0;
		if (Global.character_.isInFestival() > 0)
		{
			num2 = Global.character_.isInFestival();
		}
		else if (Global.character_.isAfterFestival() > 0)
		{
			num2 = Global.character_.isAfterFestival();
		}
		foreach (FestivalConsume item in m_fclist)
		{
			if (!item.isgot)
			{
				int num3 = (item.day - (num2 + 18000 - 1296000)) / 86400;
				int num4 = num3 % 5;
				int num5 = num3 / 5;
				GUIStyle style = ui_hongbao2;
				if (Global.character_.isInFestival() != 0 && item.day >= MainUI.get_Last_RefreshTime(TimeUtil.Now))
				{
					style = ui_hongbao1;
					flag = true;
				}
				GUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(362 + num4 * 73, 150 + num5 * 73, 78f, 78f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "store" + item.day), style);
				if (Global.character_.isAfterFestival() != 0 && ui_lingqu3.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(379 + num4 * 73, 210 + num5 * 73, 44f, 16f), string.Empty))
				{
					cs_festival_reward_get(item.day);
				}
				num++;
			}
		}
		num2 = 0;
		if (Global.character_.isInFestival() > 0)
		{
			num2 = Global.character_.isInFestival();
			if (!flag)
			{
				int num6 = (int)(MainUI.get_Last_RefreshTime(TimeUtil.Now) - (num2 + 18000 - 1296000)) / 86400;
				int num7 = num6 % 5;
				int num8 = num6 / 5;
				GUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(362 + num7 * 73, 150 + num8 * 73, 78f, 78f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "storing"), ui_hongbao1);
			}
			DateTime dateTime = new DateTime(1970, 1, 1).AddSeconds(num2 - TimeUtil.server_time_zone + 18000);
			string text = __BaseUI.load_str(20598u, dateTime.Month, dateTime.Day);
			int num9 = (int)Math.Ceiling(((double)num2 - (double)TimeUtil.server_time_zone - (double)TimeUtil.Now) / 24.0 / 3600.0);
			if (num2 - TimeUtil.server_time_zone - TimeUtil.Now > 0)
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(382f, 393f, 340f, 35f), __BaseUI.load_str(20597u, text, num9), __BaseUI._textRight, __BaseUI._clrGreen, 12);
			}
		}
		else if (Global.character_.isAfterFestival() > 0)
		{
			num2 = Global.character_.isAfterFestival();
		}
		int num10 = num2 - 1296000;
		DateTime dateTime2 = new DateTime(1970, 1, 1).AddSeconds(num10 - TimeUtil.server_time_zone);
		string text2 = __BaseUI.load_str(20598u, dateTime2.Month, dateTime2.Day);
		DateTime dateTime3 = new DateTime(1970, 1, 1).AddSeconds(num2 - TimeUtil.server_time_zone - 86400 + 28800);
		string text3 = __BaseUI.load_str(20598u, dateTime3.Month, dateTime3.Day);
		ScenarioSection scenarioSection = new ScenarioSection();
		scenarioSection.setParams(340, 80, 12);
		scenarioSection.makes_string(__BaseUI.load_str(20596u, text2, text3), __BaseUI._textNLeft);
		scenarioSection.draw(__BaseUI._rect(382f, 385f, 340f, 35f), __BaseUI._textNLeft);
	}

	public void cs_festival_reward_get(int id)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSFestivalRewardGet_0x362 cSFestivalRewardGet_0x = new CSFestivalRewardGet_0x362();
			cSFestivalRewardGet_0x.day = (uint)id;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(866, cSFestivalRewardGet_0x, sc_festival_reward_get, id);
		}
	}

	public void sc_festival_reward_get(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		int num = (int)arg;
		SCFestivalRewardGet_0x362 sCFestivalRewardGet_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCFestivalRewardGet_0x362>(stream);
		if (sCFestivalRewardGet_0x.operate != SCFestivalRewardGet_0x362.Operate.OK)
		{
			return;
		}
		List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
		foreach (Treasure bonuse in sCFestivalRewardGet_0x.bonuses)
		{
			_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
			treasure.make(bonuse);
			list.Add(treasure);
		}
		if (list.Count > 0)
		{
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, ResumeUI);
			battleResultUI.MyLoading();
		}
		if (Global.character_.festivalconsumes.ContainsKey(num))
		{
			Global.character_.festivalconsumes[num].isgot = true;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < m_fclist.Count; i++)
		{
			if (m_fclist[i].day == num)
			{
				m_fclist.RemoveAt(i);
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	public void cs_premium_gen()
	{
		int num = 0;
		foreach (FormGeneral value in Global.character_.form_generals_.Values)
		{
			if (value.template.color >= 4 && value.idgeneral_template != 0)
			{
				num++;
			}
		}
		if (num > Global.character_.purple_gen_award)
		{
			CSPremiumGen_0x338 pkt = new CSPremiumGen_0x338();
			RootUI.m_client.Send(824, pkt, sc_premium_gen);
		}
		else
		{
			__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(431u), rmg);
		}
	}

	public void sc_premium_gen(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream != null)
		{
			SCPremiumGen_0x338 sCPremiumGen_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCPremiumGen_0x338>(stream);
			switch (sCPremiumGen_0x.operate)
			{
			case SCPremiumGen_0x338.Operate.OK:
			{
				List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make((byte)sCPremiumGen_0x.type, 0, (int)sCPremiumGen_0x.amount);
				list.Add(treasure);
				BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
				battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, EnableAll);
				__BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI().PrepareLoading("nocollecting");
				__BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI().StartLoading();
				Global.character_.purple_gen_award++;
				break;
			}
			}
		}
	}

	public void sc_level_award(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		sended = false;
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		SCLevelAwardGet_0x32F sCLevelAwardGet_0x32F = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCLevelAwardGet_0x32F>(stream);
		switch (sCLevelAwardGet_0x32F.operate)
		{
		case SCLevelAwardGet_0x32F.Operate.OK:
		{
			uint level = sCLevelAwardGet_0x32F.level;
			_level_award level_award = Global.getTemplate("level_award", level) as _level_award;
			List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
			_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
			if (level_award.money > 0)
			{
				treasure.make(4, 0, level_award.money);
				list.Add(treasure);
				__BaseUI.drawStateBubble("money" + level_award.money);
			}
			if (level_award.mana > 0)
			{
				treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make(5, 0, level_award.mana);
				list.Add(treasure);
				__BaseUI.drawStateBubble("mana" + level_award.mana);
			}
			if (level_award.reputation > 0)
			{
				treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make(18, 0, level_award.reputation);
				list.Add(treasure);
			}
			if (level_award.stone > 0)
			{
				treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make(30, 0, level_award.stone);
				list.Add(treasure);
			}
			if (level_award.high_stone > 0)
			{
				treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make(63, 0, level_award.high_stone);
				list.Add(treasure);
			}
			if (level_award.enchant_stone > 0)
			{
				treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make(10, 0, level_award.enchant_stone);
				list.Add(treasure);
			}
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(null, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, closeBonus);
			battleResultUI.MyLoading();
			Global.character_.newbieinfo.award_level = (short)level;
			break;
		}
		}
	}

	private void DrawShenlong()
	{
		int spirit_beast_award = Global.character_.newbieinfo.spirit_beast_award;
		spirit_beast_award = Math.Max((int)Global.character_.newbieinfo.spirit_beast_award, 2);
		spirit_beast_award = Math.Min(spirit_beast_award, 7);
		for (int i = spirit_beast_award; i < spirit_beast_award + 4; i++)
		{
			string tooltip = string.Empty;
			if (Global.getTemplate("spirit_beast_award", (uint)i) is _spirit_beast_award spirit_beast_award2)
			{
				if (spirit_beast_award2.rank < 6)
				{
					tooltip = string.Format(__BaseUI.load_str(471u), spirit_beast_award2.money, spirit_beast_award2.reputation, spirit_beast_award2.enchant_stone);
				}
				else if (spirit_beast_award2.rank == 6)
				{
					tooltip = string.Format(__BaseUI.load_str(9025u), spirit_beast_award2.black_fruit);
				}
				else if (spirit_beast_award2.rank > 6)
				{
					tooltip = string.Format(__BaseUI.load_str(9026u), spirit_beast_award2.black_fruit, spirit_beast_award2.skill_fragment);
				}
			}
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(223 + 130 * (i - spirit_beast_award), 169f, 112f, 157f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, tooltip), ui_jiangli[i - 2], GUI.color);
			if (i <= Global.character_.newbieinfo.spirit_beast_award)
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(240 + 132 * (i - spirit_beast_award), 330f, 71f, 49f), string.Empty, ui_ling);
			}
			else if (i == Global.character_.newbieinfo.spirit_beast_award + 1 && Global.character_.spirit.idspirit_beast >= i)
			{
				if (ui_lingqu2.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(239 + 130 * (i - spirit_beast_award), 347f, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, tooltip)))
				{
					cs_get_spirit_award();
				}
				Color backgroundColor = GUI.backgroundColor;
				GUI.backgroundColor = new Color(GUI.backgroundColor.r, GUI.backgroundColor.g, GUI.backgroundColor.b, light_alpha);
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(230 + 130 * (i - spirit_beast_award), 338f, 98f, 40f), string.Empty, ui_lingqu_light);
				GUI.backgroundColor = backgroundColor;
			}
			else
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(223 + 130 * (i - spirit_beast_award), 319f, 123f, 69f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, tooltip), ui_long[i - 2], GUI.color);
			}
		}
		if ((!Global.character_.checkUI("army_spirit") && !Global.character_.checkUI("spirit2") && !MainUI.checkSpirit3("spirit3")) || !ui_qianwang2.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(528f, 400f, 202f, 29f), string.Empty))
		{
			return;
		}
		if (MainUI.checkSpirit3("spirit3"))
		{
			SpiritUI_3 component = BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpiritUI_3>();
			if (MainUI.handle_sub_ui(component, "spirit3"))
			{
				component.MyLoading();
			}
		}
		else if (Global.character_.spirit != null && Global.character_.spirit.level < 41)
		{
			SpiritUI component2 = BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpiritUI>();
			if (MainUI.handle_sub_ui(component2, "army_spirit"))
			{
				component2.MyLoading();
			}
		}
		else if (Global.character_.spirit != null && Global.character_.spirit.level >= 41)
		{
			SpiritUI_2 component3 = BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpiritUI_2>();
			if (MainUI.handle_sub_ui(component3, "spirit2"))
			{
				component3.MyLoading();
			}
		}
	}

			if (Global.getTemplate("spirit_beast_award", (uint)i) is _spirit_beast_award spirit_beast_award)
			{
				tooltip = ((spirit_beast_award.rank >= 5) ? string.Format(__BaseUI.load_str(21655u), spirit_beast_award.money, spirit_beast_award.reputation, spirit_beast_award.black_fruit) : string.Format(__BaseUI.load_str(471u), spirit_beast_award.money, spirit_beast_award.reputation, spirit_beast_award.enchant_stone));
			}
			GUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(223 + 130 * (i - num), 205f, 112f, 121f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, tooltip), __BaseUI.normalStyle);
			if (i <= Global.character_.newbieinfo.spirit_beast_award)
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(240 + 132 * (i - num), 330f, 71f, 49f), string.Empty, ui_ling);
			}
			else if (i == Global.character_.newbieinfo.spirit_beast_award + 1 && Global.character_.spirit.idspirit_beast >= i)
			{
				if (ui_lingqu2.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(239 + 130 * (i - num), 347f, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, tooltip)))
				{
					cs_get_spirit_award();
				}
				Color backgroundColor = GUI.backgroundColor;
				GUI.backgroundColor = new Color(GUI.backgroundColor.r, GUI.backgroundColor.g, GUI.backgroundColor.b, light_alpha);
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(230 + 130 * (i - num), 338f, 98f, 40f), string.Empty, ui_lingqu_light);
				GUI.backgroundColor = backgroundColor;
			}
			else
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(223 + 130 * (i - num), 319f, 123f, 69f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, tooltip), ui_long[i - 2], GUI.color);
			}
		}
		if ((!Global.character_.checkUI("army_spirit") && !Global.character_.checkUI("spirit2")) || !ui_qianwang2.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(528f, 415f, 202f, 29f), string.Empty))
		{
			return;
		}
		if (Global.character_.checkUI("army_spirit") || Global.character_.checkUI("spirit2") || MainUI.checkSpirit3("spirit3"))
		{
			if (MainUI.checkSpirit3("spirit3"))
			{
				SpiritUI_3 component = BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpiritUI_3>();
				if (MainUI.handle_sub_ui(component, "spirit3"))
				{
					component.MyLoading();
				}
			}
			if (Global.character_.spirit != null && Global.character_.spirit.level < 41)
			{
				SpiritUI component2 = BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpiritUI>();
				if (MainUI.handle_sub_ui(component2, "army_spirit"))
				{
					component2.MyLoading();
				}
			}
			else if (Global.character_.spirit != null && Global.character_.spirit.level >= 41)
			{
				SpiritUI_2 component3 = BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpiritUI_2>();
				if (MainUI.handle_sub_ui(component3, "spirit2"))
				{
					component3.MyLoading();
				}
			}
		}
		else
		{
			__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(21506u), rmg);
		}
	}

	public void cs_get_spirit_award()

	public void sc_get_spirit_award(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		sended = false;
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		SCGetSpiritAward_0x34A sCGetSpiritAward_0x34A = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCGetSpiritAward_0x34A>(stream);
		switch (sCGetSpiritAward_0x34A.operate)
		{
		case SCGetSpiritAward_0x34A.Operate.OK:
		{
			byte b = (byte)(Global.character_.newbieinfo.spirit_beast_award + 1);
			if (!(Global.getTemplate("spirit_beast_award", b) is _spirit_beast_award))
			{
				break;
			}
			List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
			foreach (Treasure bonu in sCGetSpiritAward_0x34A.bonus)
			{
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make(bonu);
				list.Add(treasure);
			}
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(null, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, closeBonus);
			battleResultUI.MyLoading();
			Global.character_.newbieinfo.spirit_beast_award = b;
			break;
		}
		}
	}

	private int checkTroop(ushort id)
	{
		if (Global.getTemplate("anecdote_reward", id) is _anecdote_reward && Global.character_.anecdoterewards.ContainsKey(id))
		{
			AnecdoteReward anecdoteReward = Global.character_.anecdoterewards[id];
			return anecdoteReward.finish;
		}
		return 0;
	}

	private void DrawTroop(int index)
	{
		if (m_arlist.Count < index)
		{
			return;
		}
		float num = 0f;
		if (leftTimer > DateTime.Now)
		{
			num = (float)Math.Sin((leftTimer - DateTime.Now).TotalSeconds / 0.5 * -1.0 * Math.PI / 2.0) * 166f * 3f;
		}
		if (rightTimer > DateTime.Now)
		{
			num = (float)Math.Sin((rightTimer - DateTime.Now).TotalSeconds / 0.5 * Math.PI / 2.0) * 166f * 3f;
		}
		int num2 = index - m_troopPage * 3;
		_anecdote_reward anecdote_reward = m_arlist[index];
		int num3 = checkTroop(anecdote_reward.id);
		bool flag = false;
		if (m_faceiddic.ContainsKey(anecdote_reward.id))
		{
			uint key = m_faceiddic[anecdote_reward.id];
			if (m_facedic.ContainsKey(key))
			{
				Texture2D texture2D = m_facedic[key];
				if (texture2D != null)
				{
					GUI.DrawTexture(__BaseUI._rect((float)(253 + num2 * 166) + num, 180f, 96f, 96f), texture2D);
					flag = true;
				}
			}
		}
		if (!flag && m_faceiddic.ContainsKey(anecdote_reward.id))
		{
			AddAssetSub("face" + m_faceiddic[anecdote_reward.id]);
			StartLoadingSub();
		}
		__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect((float)(226 + num2 * 166) + num, 120f, 153f, 242f), string.Empty, ui_di11_bars[index % 3]);
		if (Global.getTemplate("campaign", anecdote_reward.id) is _campaign campaign)
		{
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect((float)(252 + num2 * 166) + num - 20f, 274f, 140f, 22f), campaign.name, __BaseUI._textCenter, __BaseUI._clrYellow);
		}
		switch (num3)
		{
		case 2:
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect((float)(256 + num2 * 166) + num, 166f, 88f, 18f), __BaseUI.load_str(483u), __BaseUI._textCenter, __BaseUI._clrGreen);
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect((float)(272 + num2 * 166) + num, 208f, 66f, 66f), string.Empty, ui_zi112);
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect((float)(240 + num2 * 166) + num, 298f, 127f, 49f), string.Empty, ui_zi113);
			break;
		case 1:
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect((float)(256 + num2 * 166) + num, 165f, 88f, 18f), __BaseUI.load_str(483u), __BaseUI._textCenter, __BaseUI._clrGreen);
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect((float)(272 + num2 * 166) + num, 208f, 66f, 66f), string.Empty, ui_zi112);
			if (ui_lingqu2.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect((float)(265 + num2 * 166) + num, 363f, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
			{
				cs_anecdote_get_reward(anecdote_reward.id);
			}
			if (m_uiawards.ContainsKey(anecdote_reward.award_pic))
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect((float)(239 + num2 * 166) + num, 295f, 127f, 49f), string.Empty, m_uiawards[anecdote_reward.award_pic]);
			}
			break;
		case 0:
			if (MainUI.CalcServerOpenRefreshDaysTest() < anecdote_reward.days)
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect((float)(256 + num2 * 166) + num, 165f, 88f, 18f), string.Format(__BaseUI.load_str(484u), anecdote_reward.days - MainUI.CalcServerOpenRefreshDaysTest() - 1), __BaseUI._textCenter, __BaseUI._clrWhite);
			}
			else
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect((float)(256 + num2 * 166) + num, 165f, 88f, 18f), __BaseUI.load_str(482u), __BaseUI._textCenter, __BaseUI._clrRed);
			}
			if (m_uiawards.ContainsKey(anecdote_reward.award_pic))
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect((float)(239 + num2 * 166) + num, 295f, 127f, 49f), string.Empty, m_uiawards[anecdote_reward.award_pic]);
			}
			break;
		}
	}

	public void cs_anecdote_get_reward(ushort id)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSAnecdoteGetReward_0x357 cSAnecdoteGetReward_0x = new CSAnecdoteGetReward_0x357();
			cSAnecdoteGetReward_0x.id = id;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(855, cSAnecdoteGetReward_0x, sc_anecdote_get_reward, id);
		}
	}

	public void sc_anecdote_get_reward(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		ushort key = (ushort)arg;
		SCAnecdoteGetReward_0x357 sCAnecdoteGetReward_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCAnecdoteGetReward_0x357>(stream);
		if (sCAnecdoteGetReward_0x.operate != SCAnecdoteGetReward_0x357.Operate.OK)
		{
			return;
		}
		List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
		foreach (Treasure item in sCAnecdoteGetReward_0x.reward)
		{
			_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
			treasure.make(item);
			list.Add(treasure);
		}
		if (list.Count > 0)
		{
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, ResumeUI);
			battleResultUI.MyLoading();
		}
		if (Global.character_.anecdoterewards.ContainsKey(key))
		{
			Global.character_.anecdoterewards[key].finish = 2;
		}
	}

	private void DrawZhoumo()
	{
		__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(528f, 418f, 25f, 19f), Global.character_.newbieinfo.weekend_award.ToString(), __BaseUI._numCenter, __BaseUI._clrWhite);
		if (!showResult)
		{
			if (Global.character_.newbieinfo.weekend_award < 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
			{
				int num = i % 4;
				int num2 = i / 4;
				Rect rc = __BaseUI._rect(275 + num * 101, 200 + num2 * 85, 101f, 85f);
				if (ui_fudai.Button(IsEnable(), rc, string.Empty))
				{
					cs_weekend_lottery();
				}
			}
		}
		else
		{
			DrawResult();
		}
	}

	private void DrawResult()
	{
		TimeSpan timeSpan = weekendTimer - DateTime.Now;
		if (timeSpan.TotalSeconds > 0.5)
		{
			float a = (float)((timeSpan.TotalSeconds - 0.5) / 0.2);
			Color backgroundColor = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, a);
			Color backgroundColor2 = GUI.backgroundColor;
			GUI.backgroundColor = backgroundColor;
			for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
			{
				int num = i % 4;
				int num2 = i / 4;
				Rect rc = __BaseUI._rect(275 + num * 101, 200 + num2 * 85, 101f, 85f);
				__BaseUI.Label(rc, string.Empty, ui_fudai.btnStyle);
			}
			GUI.backgroundColor = backgroundColor2;
			return;
		}
		if (timeSpan.TotalSeconds > 0.0)
		{
			double num3 = 1.0 - timeSpan.TotalSeconds / 0.5;
			int num4 = (int)(217.0 * num3);
			int num5 = (int)(204.0 * num3);
			int num6 = 359 + (217 - num4) / 2;
			int num7 = 201 + (204 - num5) / 2;
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(num6, num7, num4, num5), string.Empty, ui_fudai_di);
			return;
		}
		__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(359f, 201f, 217f, 204f), string.Empty, ui_fudai_di);
		if (m_treasure != null)
		{
			if (m_treasure.texIcon != null)
			{
				GUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(441f, 251f, 50f, 50f), new GUIContent(m_treasure.texIcon, "treasure"), __BaseUI.normalStyle);
			}
			else
			{
				AddAssetSub(m_treasure.getIconName());
				StartLoadingSub();
			}
			__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(403f, 321f, 118f, 20f), "x" + m_treasure.param[1], __BaseUI._numCenter, __BaseUI._clrWhite, 18);
		}
		if (__BaseUI._btnnewOK.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(420f, 352f, 98f, 30f), string.Empty))
		{
			showResult = false;
			m_treasure = null;
		}
	}

	public void cs_weekend_lottery()
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSWeekendLottery_0x34B pkt = new CSWeekendLottery_0x34B();
			RootUI.m_client.Send(843, pkt, sc_weekend_lottery);
		}
	}

	public void sc_weekend_lottery(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream != null)
		{
			sended = false;
			SCWeekendLottery_0x34B sCWeekendLottery_0x34B = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCWeekendLottery_0x34B>(stream);
			switch (sCWeekendLottery_0x34B.operate)
			{
			case SCWeekendLottery_0x34B.Operate.OK:
			{
				List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make(sCWeekendLottery_0x34B.treasure);
				list.Add(treasure);
				Global.character_.addParam(treasure.name, treasure.param[0], treasure.param[1]);
				m_treasure = treasure;
				Global.character_.newbieinfo.weekend_award--;
				showResult = true;
				weekendTimer = DateTime.Now.AddSeconds(0.7);
				__BaseUI.drawStateBubble(treasure.name + treasure.param[1]);
				break;
			}
			case SCWeekendLottery_0x34B.Operate.Error:
				__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(480u), rmg);
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	private int calcGoldLotteryTimes()
	{
		int needgold = 0;
		int allgold = 0;
		return calcGoldLotteryTimes(out needgold, out allgold);
	}

	private int calcGoldLotteryTimes(out int needgold, out int allgold)
	{
		int gold_consume = (int)Global.character_.gold_consume;
		int num = 0;
		gold_consume -= 50;
		needgold = 50;
		allgold = 50;
		while (gold_consume >= 0)
		{
			num++;
			switch (num)
			{
			case 1:
				allgold = 75;
				break;
			case 2:
				allgold = 100;
				break;
			case 3:
				allgold = 150;
				break;
			default:
				allgold = 200;
				break;
			}
			gold_consume -= allgold;
		}
		needgold = gold_consume * -1;
		return num;
	}

	private void DrawJifen()
	{
		int needgold = 0;
		int allgold = 0;
		calcGoldLotteryTimes(out needgold, out allgold);
		if (calcGoldLotteryTimes() > Global.character_.newbieinfo.gold_consume_award)
		{
			__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(545f, 419f, 28f, 17f), (calcGoldLotteryTimes() - Global.character_.newbieinfo.gold_consume_award).ToString(), __BaseUI._numCenter, __BaseUI._clrWhite);
		}
		else
		{
			__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(545f, 419f, 28f, 17f), 0.ToString(), __BaseUI._numCenter, __BaseUI._clrWhite);
		}
		Rect rc = _CalcRect(384, 420, 69, 10, 473, 423);
		int num = (int)((float)(allgold - needgold) / (float)allgold * rc.width);
		GUI.DrawTexture(new Rect(rc.x, rc.y, num, rc.height), __BaseUI._barBlueTex);
		__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(rc, allgold - needgold + "/" + allgold, __BaseUI._numCenter, __BaseUI._clrWhite);
		if (!showResult)
		{
			if (calcGoldLotteryTimes() > Global.character_.newbieinfo.gold_consume_award)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
				{
					int num2 = i % 4;
					int num3 = i / 4;
					Rect rc2 = __BaseUI._rect(275 + num2 * 101, 200 + num3 * 85, 101f, 85f);
					if (ui_fudai.Button(IsEnable(), rc2, string.Empty))
					{
						cs_gold_lottery();
					}
				}
			}
			__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(349f, 390f, 49f, 20f), __BaseUI.load_str(472u), __BaseUI._textCenter, new Color(1f, 0.8f, 0.4f, 1f));
			__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(459f, 390f, 140f, 20f), __BaseUI.load_str(473u), __BaseUI._textCenter, new Color(1f, 0.8f, 0.4f, 1f));
			__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(396f, 390f, 65f, 20f), needgold + __BaseUI.load_str(544u), __BaseUI._textCenter, new Color(1f, 1f, 0f, 1f));
		}
		else
		{
			DrawResult();
		}
	}
