	public void sc_gold_lottery(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream != null)
		{
			sended = false;
			SCGoldLottery_0x34C sCGoldLottery_0x34C = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCGoldLottery_0x34C>(stream);
			switch (sCGoldLottery_0x34C.operate)
			{
			case SCGoldLottery_0x34C.Operate.OK:
			{
				List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make(sCGoldLottery_0x34C.treasure);
				list.Add(treasure);
				Global.character_.addParam(treasure.name, treasure.param[0], treasure.param[1]);
				m_treasure = treasure;
				Global.character_.newbieinfo.gold_consume_award++;
				showResult = true;
				weekendTimer = DateTime.Now.AddSeconds(0.7);
				__BaseUI.drawStateBubble(treasure.name + treasure.param[1]);
				break;
			}
			}
		}
	}

	private bool checkVipLevel(uint level)
	{
		foreach (_vip_rewards value in Global.getTemplates("vip_rewards").Values)
		{
			if (value.vip_level == level && !Global.character_.viprewards.Contains(value.id))
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	private bool checkVipLevelNew(uint level)
	{
		foreach (_vip_rewards value in Global.getTemplates("vip_rewards_new").Values)
		{
			if (value.vip_level == level && !Global.character_.viprewards.Contains(value.id))
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	private void DrawVipLibao()
	{
		__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(621f, 83f, 39f, 19f), "VIP" + Global.character_.VIPLEVEL, __BaseUI._numCenter, __BaseUI._clrWhite);
		if (ui_chongzhi3.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(665f, 80f, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
		{
			RootUI.Coin();
		}
		for (uint num = 1u; num <= 10; num++)
		{
			if (num > Global.character_.VIPLEVEL)
			{
				continue;
			}
			if (checkVipLevel(num))
			{
				if (!ui_lingqu2.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(664f, 118 + 32 * (num - 1), 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
				{
					continue;
				}
				bool flag = true;
				foreach (object value in Global.getTemplates("vip_rewards").Values)
				{
					_vip_rewards vip_rewards = (_vip_rewards)value;
					if ((vip_rewards.idtreasure_type == 42 || vip_rewards.idtreasure_type == 42 || vip_rewards.idtreasure_type == 54) && !canBuySpItem(vip_rewards.idtreasure_type, vip_rewards.param0, (int)vip_rewards.param1))
					{
						flag = false;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					cs_vip_get_reward(num);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(687f, 115 + 32 * (num - 1), 27f, 26f), string.Empty, ui_gou);
			}
		}
	}

	private void DrawVipLibaoNew()
	{
		__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(621f, 83f, 39f, 19f), "VIP" + Global.character_.VIPLEVEL, __BaseUI._numCenter, __BaseUI._clrWhite);
		if (ui_chongzhi3.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(665f, 80f, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
		{
			RootUI.Coin();
		}
		for (uint num = 1u; num <= 10; num++)
		{
			if (num > Global.character_.VIPLEVEL)
			{
				continue;
			}
			if (checkVipLevelNew(num))
			{
				if (!ui_lingqu2.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(664f, 118 + 32 * (num - 1), 80f, 22f), string.Empty) || !ui_lingqu2.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(664f, 118 + 32 * (num - 1), 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
				{
					continue;
				}
				bool flag = true;
				foreach (object value in Global.getTemplates("vip_rewards_new").Values)
				{
					_vip_rewards vip_rewards = (_vip_rewards)value;
					if ((vip_rewards.idtreasure_type == 42 || vip_rewards.idtreasure_type == 42 || vip_rewards.idtreasure_type == 54) && !canBuySpItem(vip_rewards.idtreasure_type, vip_rewards.param0, (int)vip_rewards.param1))
					{
						flag = false;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					cs_vip_get_reward_new(num);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(687f, 115 + 32 * (num - 1), 27f, 26f), string.Empty, ui_gou);
			}
		}
	}

	public void cs_vip_get_reward(uint level)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSVipGetReward_0x356 cSVipGetReward_0x = new CSVipGetReward_0x356();
			cSVipGetReward_0x.level = level;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(854, cSVipGetReward_0x, sc_vip_get_reward);
		}
	}

	public void sc_vip_get_reward(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCVipGetReward_0x356 sCVipGetReward_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCVipGetReward_0x356>(stream);
		if (sCVipGetReward_0x.operate != SCVipGetReward_0x356.Operate.OK)
		{
			return;
		}
		uint num = 0u;
		List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
		foreach (uint id in sCVipGetReward_0x.ids)
		{
			if (!Global.character_.viprewards.Contains(id))
			{
				Global.character_.viprewards.Add(id);
			}
		}
		foreach (Treasure item in sCVipGetReward_0x.reward)
		{
			if (item.idtreasure_type != 44)
			{
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make(item);
				list.Add(treasure);
			}
			else if (num < item.param1)
			{
				num = (uint)item.param1;
			}
		}
		if (list.Count > 0)
		{
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, ResumeUI);
			battleResultUI.MyLoading();
		}
		if (num != 0)
		{
			bool flag = true;
			foreach (Formation value in Global.character_.formations_.Values)
			{
				if (value.availability && value.spotnum >= num)
				{
					flag = false;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				SpecialGetUI component = BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpecialGetUI>();
				component.ShowSPGet_Spot(num);
				MainUI.SetSubUI(component);
			}
			else
			{
				__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.get_string("vip_noformslot").Replace(":num:", num.ToString()), rmg, ResumeUI);
			}
		}
		if (list.Count == 0 && num == 0)
		{
			__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.get_string("vip_noreward"), rmg);
		}
	}

	public void cs_vip_get_reward_new(uint level)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSVipGetRewardNew_0x3AB cSVipGetRewardNew_0x3AB = new CSVipGetRewardNew_0x3AB();
			cSVipGetRewardNew_0x3AB.level = level;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(939, cSVipGetRewardNew_0x3AB, sc_vip_get_reward_new);
		}
	}

	public void sc_vip_get_reward_new(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCVipGetRewardNew_0x3AB sCVipGetRewardNew_0x3AB = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCVipGetRewardNew_0x3AB>(stream);
		if (sCVipGetRewardNew_0x3AB.operate != SCVipGetRewardNew_0x3AB.Operate.OK)
		{
			return;
		}
		uint num = 0u;
		List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
		foreach (uint id in sCVipGetRewardNew_0x3AB.ids)
		{
			if (!Global.character_.viprewards.Contains(id))
			{
				Global.character_.viprewards.Add(id);
			}
		}
		foreach (Treasure item in sCVipGetRewardNew_0x3AB.reward)
		{
			if (item.idtreasure_type != 44)
			{
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make(item);
				list.Add(treasure);
			}
			else if (num < item.param1)
			{
				num = (uint)item.param1;
			}
		}
		if (list.Count > 0)
		{
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, ResumeUI);
			battleResultUI.MyLoading();
		}
		if (num != 0)
		{
			bool flag = true;
			foreach (Formation value in Global.character_.formations_.Values)
			{
				if (value.availability && value.spotnum >= num)
				{
					flag = false;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				SpecialGetUI component = BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpecialGetUI>();
				component.ShowSPGet_Spot(num);
				MainUI.SetSubUI(component);
			}
			else
			{
				__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.get_string("vip_noformslot").Replace(":num:", num.ToString()), rmg, ResumeUI);
			}
		}
		if (list.Count == 0 && num == 0)
		{
			__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.get_string("vip_noreward"), rmg);
		}
	}

	private void DrawTongguan()
	{
		__BaseUI.BeginGroup(__BaseUI._rect(228f, 0f, 485f, h));
		__BaseUI.BeginGroup(__BaseUI._rect(-228f, 0f, w, h));
		for (int i = 0; i < m_arlist.Count; i++)
		{
			if (leftTimer > DateTime.Now && i / 3 == m_troopPage + 1)
			{
				DrawTroop(i);
			}
			if (rightTimer > DateTime.Now && i / 3 == m_troopPage - 1)
			{
				DrawTroop(i);
			}
			if (i / 3 == m_troopPage)
			{
				DrawTroop(i);
			}
		}
		__BaseUI.EndGroup();
		__BaseUI.EndGroup();
		int num = m_arlist.Count / 3;
		if (m_troopPage > 0 && __BaseUI._btnPageDown5.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(179f, 227f, 48f, 61f), string.Empty))
		{
			m_troopPage--;
			leftTimer = DateTime.Now.AddSeconds(0.5);
		}
		if (m_troopPage < num && __BaseUI._btnPageUp5.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(718f, 227f, 48f, 61f), string.Empty))
		{
			m_troopPage++;
			rightTimer = DateTime.Now.AddSeconds(0.5);
		}
		if (Global.character_.checkUI("anecdote") && ui_qianwang.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(399f, 398f, 146f, 36f), string.Empty))
		{
			AnecdoteSelectUI component = GameObject.Find("Anecdote/AnecdoteUI").GetComponent<AnecdoteSelectUI>();
			if (MainUI.handle_sub_ui(component, "Anecdote"))
			{
				rmg.ToDisable();
				component.SetCampaigns();
			}
		}
	}

	private void DrawXinshouka()
	{
		Rect textRc = __BaseUI._rect(400f, 257f, 305f, 18f);
		strCode = __BaseUI.TextField(textRc, strCode, 256, __BaseUI._textCenter, __BaseUI._clrWhite);
		strCode = strCode.Trim().Replace("\n", string.Empty);
		if (__BaseUI._btnOK.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(502f, 297f, 98f, 30f), string.Empty) && !xinshoukasent)
		{
			if (strCode.Length == 0)
			{
				strResult = __BaseUI.load_str(485u);
				__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, strResult, rmg);
			}
			else if (strCode.ToLower() == LoginUI.getNewbieCode())
			{
				CSNewbieGift_0x32 pkt = new CSNewbieGift_0x32();
				RootUI.m_client.Send(50, pkt, sc_newbie_gift);
				xinshoukasent = true;
			}
			else
			{
				CSCashGift_0x230 cSCashGift_0x = new CSCashGift_0x230();
				cSCashGift_0x.code = strCode;
				RootUI.m_client.Send(560, cSCashGift_0x, sc_cash_gift);
				xinshoukasent = true;
			}
		}
		if (Global.game_data.Contains("newbie_url") && Global.game_data["newbie_url"] != null && Global.game_data["newbie_url"].ToString().Length > 0 && ui_jihuoma.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(541f, 405f, 202f, 29f), string.Empty))
		{
			string url = Global.game_data["newbie_url"].ToString();
			Global.IEExplore(url, "newbie");
		}
	}

	private void sc_newbie_gift(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		xinshoukasent = false;
		rmg.ToEnable();
		if (stream != null)
		{
			SCNewbieGift_0x32 sCNewbieGift_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCNewbieGift_0x32>(stream);
			if (sCNewbieGift_0x.result == 0)
			{
				Global.character_.newbie_gift = true;
				strCode = string.Empty;
				__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(486u), rmg);
			}
			else if (sCNewbieGift_0x.result == 1)
			{
				__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(495u), rmg);
			}
		}
	}

	private void sc_cash_gift(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		xinshoukasent = false;
		rmg.ToEnable();
		if (stream != null)
		{
			SCCashGiftResult_0x230 sCCashGiftResult_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCCashGiftResult_0x230>(stream);
			switch (sCCashGiftResult_0x.result)
			{
			case SCCashGiftResult_0x230.ResultType.SUCCESS:
				strCode = string.Empty;
				strResult = __BaseUI.load_str(21364u, sCCashGiftResult_0x.gift_name);
				Global.character_.newbie_gift = sCCashGiftResult_0x.newbie_gift;
				break;
			case SCCashGiftResult_0x230.ResultType.ERR_CODE:
				strResult = __BaseUI.load_str(21365u);
				break;
			case SCCashGiftResult_0x230.ResultType.ERR_NAME:
				strResult = __BaseUI.load_str(21366u);
				break;
			case SCCashGiftResult_0x230.ResultType.USED_CODE:
				strResult = __BaseUI.load_str(21367u);
				break;
			case SCCashGiftResult_0x230.ResultType.USED_GIFT:
				strResult = __BaseUI.load_str(21368u);
				break;
			case SCCashGiftResult_0x230.ResultType.EXPIRED:
				strResult = __BaseUI.load_str(21369u);
				break;
			}
			__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, strResult, rmg);
		}
	}

	private bool checkQuestNeed(int questID)
	{
		if (Global.getTemplate("premium_quest", (uint)questID) is _premium_quest { need_treasure: var need_treasure } premium_quest)
		{
			return need_treasure switch
			{
				4u => Global.character_.money >= premium_quest.need_amount, 
				10u => Global.character_.enchant_stone >= premium_quest.need_amount, 
				39u => Global.character_.generalsoul_purple >= premium_quest.need_amount, 
				40u => Global.character_.generalsoul_orange >= premium_quest.need_amount, 
				_ => false, 
			};
		}
		return false;
	}

	private void seeGenDetail(uint genid)
	{
		GeneralRecruitUI component = BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<GeneralRecruitUI>();
		if (MainUI.IsSubUI(component))
		{
			MainUI.SetSubUI(null);
			return;
		}
		component.SubLoading(genid, MainUI._premium);
		MainUI.SetSubUI(component);
	}

	private bool doHaveGeneral(uint genid)
	{
		_general_origin general_origin = Global.getTemplate("general_origin", genid) as _general_origin;
		List<ushort> list = new List<ushort>();
		if (general_origin != null)
		{
			Hashtable templates = Global.getTemplates("general_origin");
			foreach (DictionaryEntry item in templates)
			{
				_general_origin general_origin2 = item.Value as _general_origin;
				if (general_origin2.origin == general_origin.origin && general_origin2.form > general_origin.form)
				{
					list.Add(general_origin2.idgeneral_template);
				}
			}
		}
		list.Add((ushort)genid);
		foreach (KeyValuePair<ushort, FormGeneral> item2 in Global.character_.form_generals_)
		{
			if (list.Contains(item2.Value.idgeneral_template))
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		foreach (GeneralsSimple item3 in Global.character_.GeneralsOutSquad)
		{
			if (list.Contains((ushort)item3.idtemplate))
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	private void DrawMingyue()
	{
		if (GUI.Button(__BaseUI._rect(198f, 165f, 302f, 219f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "generalR211"), mingyueHover))
		{
			seeGenDetail(211u);
		}
		__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(203f, 374f, 43f, 31f), string.Empty, ui_xin1);
		__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(446f, 374f, 43f, 31f), string.Empty, ui_xin2);
		__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(231f, 390f, curBarWidth, 23f), string.Empty, ui_haogan);
		if (curBarWidth < 231f)
		{
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(280f, 384f, 75f, 34f), string.Empty, ui_zi_hgd);
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(357f, 384f, 75f, 34f), friendShip + "%", __BaseUI._numLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite, 25, FontStyle.Italic);
		}
		else
		{
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(314f, 392f, 69f, 19f), string.Empty, ui_zi_ysj);
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
		{
			if (!(Global.getTemplate("premium_quest", (uint)(i + 1)) is _premium_quest premium_quest))
			{
				continue;
			}
			_treasure_type treasure_type = Global.getTemplate("treasure_type", premium_quest.need_treasure) as _treasure_type;
			_treasure_type treasure_type2 = Global.getTemplate("treasure_type", premium_quest.award_treasure) as _treasure_type;
			if (i == Global.character_.quest22)
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(497f, 195 + 75 * i, 242f, 74f), string.Empty, ui_haoganbar1);
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(522f, 201 + 75 * i, 171f, 16f), string.Empty, ui_zi_quest[i]);
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(522f, 241 + 75 * i, 200f, 16f), __BaseUI.load_str(20629u) + premium_quest.need_amount + treasure_type.desc, __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrCyan, 12);
				if (i == 2)
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(522f, 221 + 75 * i, 200f, 16f), __BaseUI.load_str(20633u), __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrGreen, 12);
					if (Global.getTemplate("general_template", (uint)premium_quest.award_param) is _general_template general_template)
					{
						__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(557f, 221 + 75 * i, 200f, 16f), general_template.name, __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrQualitys[general_template.color], 12);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(522f, 221 + 75 * i, 200f, 16f), __BaseUI.load_str(20633u) + premium_quest.award_amount + treasure_type2.desc, __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrGreen, 12);
				}
				if (checkQuestNeed(i + 1))
				{
					if (btnPay.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(643f, 236 + 75 * i, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
					{
						cs_mingyue_quest();
					}
				}
				else if (btnGoldPay.Button(Global.character_.VIPLEVEL >= 3, __BaseUI._rect(643f, 236 + 75 * i, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "MingYueGoldPay")))
				{
					__BaseUI.GetConsumeUI().setConsume(premium_quest.gold_cost, 45, GoldQuestOK);
				}
				continue;
			}
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(497f, 195 + 75 * i, 242f, 74f), string.Empty, ui_haoganbar2);
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(522f, 201 + 75 * i, 171f, 16f), string.Empty, ui_zi_quest[i]);
			if (i < Global.character_.quest22)
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(522f, 221 + 75 * i, 200f, 16f), __BaseUI.load_str(20624u) + __BaseUI.load_str(20628u), __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrGreen, 12);
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(687f, 198 + 75 * i, 40f, 40f), string.Empty, ui_zi_wc);
				continue;
			}
			if (i == 2)
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(522f, 221 + 75 * i, 200f, 16f), __BaseUI.load_str(20633u), __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrGreen, 12);
				if (Global.getTemplate("general_template", (uint)premium_quest.award_param) is _general_template general_template2)
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(557f, 221 + 75 * i, 200f, 16f), general_template2.name, __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrQualitys[general_template2.color], 12);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(522f, 221 + 75 * i, 200f, 16f), __BaseUI.load_str(20633u) + premium_quest.award_amount + treasure_type2.desc, __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrGreen, 12);
			}
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(497f, 195 + 75 * i, 242f, 74f), string.Empty, ui_haoganbar3);
		}
	}

	public void cs_mingyue_quest()
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSMingyueQuest_0x365 pkt = new CSMingyueQuest_0x365();
			RootUI.m_client.Send(869, pkt, sc_mingyue_quest);
		}
	}

	public void sc_mingyue_quest(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCMingyueQuest_0x365 sCMingyueQuest_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCMingyueQuest_0x365>(stream);
		switch (sCMingyueQuest_0x.operate)
		{
		case SCMingyueQuest_0x365.Operate.OK:
			Global.character_.quest22 = (byte)sCMingyueQuest_0x.step;
			if (Global.getTemplate("premium_quest", Global.character_.quest22) is _premium_quest premium_quest)
			{
				friendShip += premium_quest.friendship;
				newBarWidth = (float)friendShip / 100f * 231f;
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make((byte)premium_quest.need_treasure, 0, premium_quest.need_amount);
				Global.character_.remParam(treasure.name, treasure.param[1]);
				treasure.make((byte)premium_quest.award_treasure, premium_quest.award_param, premium_quest.award_amount);
				List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
				list.Add(treasure);
				if (premium_quest.award_treasure != 0)
				{
					__BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI().Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, EnableAll);
					__BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI().MyLoading();
				}
				else
				{
					BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpecialGetUI>().ShowSPGet_General((uint)treasure.param[0]);
				}
			}
			break;
		}
	}

	private void GoldQuestOK(bool bOK, object[] objs)

	public void cs_mingyue_quest_gold()
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSMingyueQuestGold_0x367 pkt = new CSMingyueQuestGold_0x367();
			RootUI.m_client.Send(871, pkt, sc_mingyue_quest_gold);
		}
	}

	public void sc_mingyue_quest_gold(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCMingyueQuestGold_0x367 sCMingyueQuestGold_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCMingyueQuestGold_0x367>(stream);
		switch (sCMingyueQuestGold_0x.operate)
		{
		case SCMingyueQuestGold_0x367.Operate.OK:
			Global.character_.quest22 = (byte)sCMingyueQuestGold_0x.step;
			if (Global.getTemplate("premium_quest", Global.character_.quest22) is _premium_quest premium_quest)
			{
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				Global.character_.gold -= premium_quest.gold_cost;
				__BaseUI.drawStateBubble("gold-" + premium_quest.gold_cost);
				friendShip += premium_quest.friendship;
				newBarWidth = (float)friendShip / 100f * 231f;
				treasure.make((byte)premium_quest.award_treasure, premium_quest.award_param, premium_quest.award_amount);
				List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
				list.Add(treasure);
				if (premium_quest.award_treasure != 0)
				{
					__BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI().Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, EnableAll);
					__BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI().MyLoading();
				}
				else
				{
					BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpecialGetUI>().ShowSPGet_General((uint)treasure.param[0]);
				}
			}
			break;
		}
	}

	private void DrawTiaozhan()

		if (ui_qianwang.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(585f, 383f, 146f, 36f), string.Empty))

			AnecdoteSelectUI component = GameObject.Find("Anecdote/AnecdoteUI").GetComponent<AnecdoteSelectUI>();
			if (MainUI.handle_sub_ui(component, "Anecdote"))
			{
				rmg.ToDisable();
				component.SetCampaigns();
			}
		}
	}

	public bool checkZhaoZiLong()
	{
		if (Global.character_.server_open_time < 1407513600)
		{
			if (Global.is_premium_new() && !MainUI.checkUI("premium_zhaozilong"))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (Global.character_.quest69 >= 5)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public bool checkZhouNianFanLiAward()
	{
		if (Global.character_.isAfterAnniersary() != 0 && Global.character_.festivalconsumes.Count > 0)
		{
			foreach (FestivalConsume value in Global.character_.festivalconsumes.Values)
			{
				if (!value.isgot)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}
		return false;
	}

	private void DrawZhaoZiLong()
	{
		Rect rc = new Rect(511f, 68f, 259f, 383f);
		if (GUITOScreen(rc).Contains(new Vector2(Input.mousePosition.x, (float)Screen.height - Input.mousePosition.y)) && GUI.Button(__BaseUI._rect(424f, 68f, 349f, 383f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "generalR234"), zilongHover))
		{
			seeGenDetail(234u);
		}
		if (!MainUI.checkUI("army_recruit"))
		{
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(214f, 283f, 291f, 50f), string.Empty, ui_bar_4);
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(258f, 300f, 182f, 15f), string.Empty, ui_zi_zm);
		}
		else
		{
			if (!(Global.getTemplate("premium_general_quest", 69u) is _premium_general_quest premium_general_quest))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (Global.character_.quest69 == 4)
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(233f, 249f, 275f, 88f), string.Empty, ui_bar_5);
				if (recruitBtn.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(357f, 297f, 98f, 30f), string.Empty))
				{
					cs_zhaoyun_quest();
				}
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < questDescs.Length && Global.getTemplate("premium_quest", (uint)(i + premium_general_quest.quest_startid)) is _premium_quest; i++)
			{
				if (i == Global.character_.quest69)
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(214f, 293 + step * i, 291f, 28f), string.Empty, ui_bar_3);
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(240f, 293 + step * i, 50f, 28f), __BaseUI.load_str(20854u) + (i + 1), __BaseUI._textLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite, __BaseUI._fontSize);
					questDescs[i].draw(__BaseUI._rect(290f, 299 + step * i, 150f, 28f), __BaseUI._textNLeft);
					if (Global.character_.quest69 == 0)
					{
						if (Global.character_.level >= 70)
						{
							if (ui_lingqu2.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
							{
								cs_zhaoyun_quest();
							}
						}
						else if (btnGoldPay.Button(Global.character_.VIPLEVEL >= 5, __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "goldzhaozilong")))
						{
							ZhaoyunGoldCost();
						}
					}
					else if (Global.character_.quest69 == 1)
					{
						if (doHaveGeneral(69u))
						{
							if (ui_lingqu2.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
							{
								cs_zhaoyun_quest();
							}
						}
						else if (btnGoldPay.Button(Global.character_.VIPLEVEL >= 5, __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "goldzhaozilong")))
						{
							ZhaoyunGoldCost();
						}
					}
					else
					{
						if (Global.character_.quest69 != 2 && Global.character_.quest69 != 3)
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (checkQuestNeed(Global.character_.quest69 + premium_general_quest.quest_startid))
						{
							if (btnPay.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
							{
								cs_zhaoyun_quest();
							}
						}
						else if (btnGoldPay.Button(Global.character_.VIPLEVEL >= 5, __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "goldzhaozilong")))
						{
							ZhaoyunGoldCost();
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(240f, 293 + step * i, 50f, 28f), __BaseUI.load_str(20854u) + (i + 1), __BaseUI._textLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite, __BaseUI._fontSize);
					questDescs[i].draw(__BaseUI._rect(290f, 299 + step * i, 150f, 28f), __BaseUI._textNLeft);
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(214f, 293 + step * i, 291f, 28f), string.Empty, ui_bar_4);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void ZhaoyunGoldCost()
	{
		if (Global.getTemplate("premium_general_quest", 69u) is _premium_general_quest premium_general_quest && Global.getTemplate("premium_quest", (uint)(Global.character_.quest69 + premium_general_quest.quest_startid)) is _premium_quest premium_quest)
		{
			__BaseUI.GetConsumeUI().setConsume(premium_quest.gold_cost, 54, ZhaoyunGoldOK);
		}
	}

	public void cs_zhaoyun_quest()
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSZhaoyunQuest_0x376 pkt = new CSZhaoyunQuest_0x376();
			RootUI.m_client.Send(886, pkt, sc_zhaoyun_quest);
		}
	}

	public void sc_zhaoyun_quest(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCZhaoyunQuest_0x376 sCZhaoyunQuest_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCZhaoyunQuest_0x376>(stream);
		switch (sCZhaoyunQuest_0x.operate)
		{
		case SCZhaoyunQuest_0x376.Operate.OK:
		{
			Global.character_.quest69 = (byte)sCZhaoyunQuest_0x.step;
			if (Global.getTemplate("premium_general_quest", 69u) is _premium_general_quest premium_general_quest && Global.getTemplate("premium_quest", (uint)(Global.character_.quest69 - 1 + premium_general_quest.quest_startid)) is _premium_quest premium_quest)
			{
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make((byte)premium_quest.need_treasure, 0, premium_quest.need_amount);
				Global.character_.remParam(treasure.name, treasure.param[1]);
			}
			List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
			foreach (Treasure bonuse in sCZhaoyunQuest_0x.bonuses)
			{
				if (bonuse.idtreasure_type == 0 || bonuse.idtreasure_type == 36)
				{
					_Treasure treasure2 = new _Treasure();
					treasure2.make(bonuse);
					BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpecialGetUI>().ShowSPGet(treasure2);
				}
				else
				{
					_Treasure treasure3 = new _Treasure();
					treasure3.make(bonuse);
					list.Add(treasure3);
				}
			}
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, EnableAll);
			battleResultUI.MyLoading();
			if (Global.character_.quest69 != 5)
			{
				break;
			}
			initPremiumPages();
			for (int i = 0; i < 54; i++)
			{
				if (checkDay[pageidx[i]] != null && checkDay[pageidx[i]]())
				{
					AddAssetSub("premium/ui_di" + (i + 1));
				}
			}
			StartLoadingSub();
			for (int j = 0; j < pageList.Count; j++)
			{
				if (pageList[j] == pageidx[19])
				{
					curPage = j;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		}
	}

		if (bOK)
		{
			cs_zhaoyun_quest_gold();
		}

		switch (sCZhaoyunQuestGold_0x.operate)
		{
		case SCZhaoyunQuestGold_0x377.Operate.OK:

			Global.character_.quest69 = (byte)sCZhaoyunQuestGold_0x.step;
			if (Global.getTemplate("premium_general_quest", 69u) is _premium_general_quest premium_general_quest && Global.getTemplate("premium_quest", (uint)(Global.character_.quest69 - 1 + premium_general_quest.quest_startid)) is _premium_quest premium_quest)

					_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();

	private void DrawHequOnline()
	{
		int num = (int)MainUI.CalcServerHequDays();
		if (num >= 3)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (Global.character_.level < 60 && (bool)texHequBonus)
		{
			GUI.DrawTexture(__BaseUI._rect(255f, 227f, 414f, 66f), texHequBonus);
		}
		if (Global.character_.getCharacterBonus("hequ_online", (num + 1).ToString(), string.Empty) != null && TimeUtil.Now > Global.character_.server_hequ_time + num * 86400)
		{
			num++;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
		{
			if (i == num)
			{
				if (texHequArrow != null)
				{
					GUI.DrawTexture(__BaseUI._rect(302 + i * 150, 292f, 42f, 22f), texHequArrow);
				}
				if (Global.character_.getCharacterBonus("hequ_online", (i + 1).ToString(), string.Empty) == null)
				{
					if (btnHequGet.Button(Global.character_.hequ_online_gift == 0, __BaseUI._rect(275 + i * 150, 376f, 94f, 36f), string.Empty))
					{
						CSBonusGet_0x4E0 cSBonusGet_0x4E = new CSBonusGet_0x4E0();
						cSBonusGet_0x4E.type = 1u;
						RootUI.m_client.Send(1248, cSBonusGet_0x4E, sc_bonus_get_0x4E0);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					btnHequGet.Button(bBtn: false, __BaseUI._rect(275 + i * 150, 376f, 94f, 36f), string.Empty);
				}
			}
			else if (Global.character_.getCharacterBonus("hequ_online", (i + 1).ToString(), string.Empty) != null)
			{
				btnHequGet.Button(bBtn: false, __BaseUI._rect(275 + i * 150, 376f, 94f, 36f), string.Empty);
			}
		}
	}

	public void sc_bonus_get_0x4E0(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		SCBonusGet_0x4E0 sCBonusGet_0x4E = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCBonusGet_0x4E0>(stream);
		if (sCBonusGet_0x4E.result == 1)
		{
			if (sCBonusGet_0x4E.type == 1)
			{
				BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
				battleResultUI.Set(string.Empty, sCBonusGet_0x4E.bonuses.bonuses, EnableAll);
				__BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI().PrepareLoading("nocollecting");
				__BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI().StartLoading();
				Global.character_.hequ_online_gift = 1;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, "failed", null);
		}
	}

		if (btnHequChongzhi.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(580f, 395f, 139f, 40f), string.Empty))
		{
			RootUI.Coin();
		}
	}

	private void DrawWanMeiShiLian()
	{
		int num = MainUI.CalcServerOpenRefreshDays();
		for (int i = 0; i < 14; i++)
		{
			if (i > num)
			{
				continue;
			}
			int num2 = i / 7 * 274;
			int num3 = i % 7 * 33;
			if (Global.character_.trialawards.ContainsKey((byte)i))
			{
				if (Global.character_.trialawards[(byte)i].finished == 2)
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(406 + num2, 193 + num3, 27f, 26f), string.Empty, ui_gou);
				}
				else if (Global.character_.trialawards[(byte)i].finished == 1)
				{
					if (i != num)
					{
						__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(408 + num2, 195 + num3, 23f, 22f), string.Empty, ui_wanmeishilian_x);
					}
					else if (ui_lingqu2.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(383 + num2, 195 + num3, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, string.Empty)))
					{
						cs_premium_trial_award((byte)i, isgold: false);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (i != num)
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(408 + num2, 195 + num3, 23f, 22f), string.Empty, ui_wanmeishilian_x);
			}
			else
			{
				if (!btnGoTrial.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(383 + num2, 195 + num3, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, string.Empty)))
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (MainUI.checkUI("training"))
				{
					TrialUI component = BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<TrialUI>();
					if (MainUI.handle_sub_ui(component, "training"))
					{
						MainUI.SetSubUI(component);
						component.cs_trial_load_all();
					}
				}
				else
				{
					__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(9027u), rmg);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void trialBounsTakeBack(bool bOK, object[] arg)
	{
		if (bOK)
		{
			byte day = 0;
			if (arg[0] != null)
			{
				day = (byte)arg[0];
			}
			cs_premium_trial_award(day, isgold: true);
		}
	}

	public void cs_premium_trial_award(byte day, bool isgold)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSPremiumTrialAward_0x38F cSPremiumTrialAward_0x38F = new CSPremiumTrialAward_0x38F();
			cSPremiumTrialAward_0x38F.day = day;
			cSPremiumTrialAward_0x38F.isgold = isgold;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(911, cSPremiumTrialAward_0x38F, sc_premium_trial_award, day);
		}
	}

	public void sc_premium_trial_award(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		byte b = (byte)arg;
		SCPremiumTrialAward_0x38F sCPremiumTrialAward_0x38F = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCPremiumTrialAward_0x38F>(stream);
		if (sCPremiumTrialAward_0x38F.result == 1)
		{
			if (Global.character_.trialawards.ContainsKey(b))
			{
				Global.character_.trialawards[b].finished = 2;
			}
			else
			{
				ServerOpenTrialAward serverOpenTrialAward = new ServerOpenTrialAward();
				serverOpenTrialAward.day = b;
				serverOpenTrialAward.finished = 2;
				Global.character_.trialawards.Add(b, serverOpenTrialAward);
			}
			if (sCPremiumTrialAward_0x38F.exp != 0)
			{
				List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make(8, 0, (int)sCPremiumTrialAward_0x38F.exp);
				list.Add(treasure);
				BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
				battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, EnableAll);
				__BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI().PrepareLoading("nocollecting");
				__BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI().StartLoading();
			}
			if (sCPremiumTrialAward_0x38F.cost != 0)
			{
				Global.character_.gold -= (int)sCPremiumTrialAward_0x38F.cost;
				__BaseUI.drawStateBubble("gold-" + sCPremiumTrialAward_0x38F.cost);
			}
		}
	}

	private void DrawZiJiangFangSong()
	{
		int num = 0;
		Hashtable templates = Global.getTemplates("purple_gen_free");
		foreach (object value in templates.Values)
		{
			if (value is _purple_gen_free purple_gen_free && doHaveGeneral(purple_gen_free.origin))
			{
				num++;
			}
		}
		if (Global.character_.login_days >= 2)
		{
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(342f, 165f, 128f, 70f), string.Empty, ui_leiji2);
			if (Global.character_.login_days >= 3)
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(474f, 165f, 79f, 70f), string.Empty, ui_leiji3);
			}
		}
		if (num < templates.Count)
		{
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(187f, 259f, 383f, 172f), string.Empty, ui_zijiangfangsong_1);
			int num2 = 0;
			Hashtable templates2 = Global.getTemplates("purple_gen_free");
			{
				foreach (object value2 in templates2.Values)
				{
					if (value2 is _purple_gen_free purple_gen_free2 && Global.getTemplate("general_template", purple_gen_free2.idgeneral_template) is _general_template general_template)
					{
						if (purpleGenSty.ContainsKey(general_template.icon))
						{
							__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(246 + 102 * num2, 315f, 58f, 58f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "generalR_noclick" + general_template.id), purpleGenSty[general_template.icon], GUI.color);
							__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(246 + 102 * num2, 315f, 58f, 58f), string.Empty, ui_zijiangfangsong_3);
						}
						else
						{
							AddAssetSub("face" + general_template.icon);
							StartLoadingSub();
						}
						if (doHaveGeneral(purple_gen_free2.origin))
						{
							__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(240 + 102 * num2, 365f, 75f, 45f), string.Empty, ui_yizhaomu);
						}
						else if (Global.character_.purple_gen_free == 0 && Global.character_.login_days >= 3 && btnlingqu.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(236 + 102 * num2, 390f, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, string.Empty)))
						{
							cs_premium_purple_gen_free(purple_gen_free2.origin, isgen: true);
						}
					}
					num2++;
				}
				return;
			}
		}
		__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(186f, 259f, 380f, 176f), string.Empty, ui_zijiangfangsong_2);
		if (Global.character_.purple_gen_free == 0 && Global.character_.login_days >= 3 && btnlingqu.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(338f, 395f, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, string.Empty)))
		{
			cs_premium_purple_gen_free(0, isgen: false);
		}
	}

	public void cs_premium_purple_gen_free(ushort origin, bool isgen)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSPremuimPurpleGenFree_0x390 cSPremuimPurpleGenFree_0x = new CSPremuimPurpleGenFree_0x390();
			cSPremuimPurpleGenFree_0x.origin = origin;
			cSPremuimPurpleGenFree_0x.gen = isgen;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(912, cSPremuimPurpleGenFree_0x, sc_premium_purple_gen_free, origin);
		}
	}

	public void sc_premium_purple_gen_free(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream != null)
		{
			sended = false;
			ushort num = (ushort)arg;
			SCPremuimPurpleGenFree_0x390 sCPremuimPurpleGenFree_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCPremuimPurpleGenFree_0x390>(stream);
			if (sCPremuimPurpleGenFree_0x.result == 1)
			{
				Global.character_.purple_gen_free = 1;
				SpecialGetUI component = BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpecialGetUI>();
				component.ShowSPGet_General((uint)sCPremuimPurpleGenFree_0x.bonus.param0);
			}
			else if (sCPremuimPurpleGenFree_0x.result == 2)
			{
				List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make(sCPremuimPurpleGenFree_0x.bonus);
				list.Add(treasure);
				BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
				battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, EnableAll);
				__BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI().PrepareLoading("nocollecting");
				__BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI().StartLoading();
				Global.character_.purple_gen_free = 1;
			}
		}
	}

	private void DrawZhouNianFanLi()
	{

		{

				num++;
			}
		}
		int num2 = 0;
		int num3 = 0;
		bool flag = false;
		foreach (FestivalConsume item2 in m_fclist)
		{
			if (Global.character_.isInAnniersary() > 0)
			{
				num3 = Global.character_.isInAnniersary();
			}
			else if (Global.character_.isAfterAnniersary() > 0)
			{
				num3 = Global.character_.isAfterAnniersary();
			}
			if (item2.isgot)
			{
				continue;
			}
			int num4 = (item2.day - (num3 + 18000 - 1296000)) / 86400;
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(redPacketPos[num2].x, redPacketPos[num2].y, 48f, 63f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "anniversarystore" + item2.day), ui_zhounian_hongbao, GUI.color);
			if (Global.character_.isInAnniersary() != 0 && item2.day >= MainUI.get_Last_RefreshTime(TimeUtil.Now))
			{
				flag = true;
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(redPacketPos[num2].x + 17f, redPacketPos[num2].y + 2f, 13f, 52f), string.Empty, ui_fanli_leijizhong);
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(redPacketPos[num2].x - 4f, redPacketPos[num2].y - 9f, 56f, 73f), string.Empty, ui_fanli_hover);
			}
			else
			{
				if (Global.character_.isAfterAnniersary() != 0)
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(redPacketPos[num2].x + 9f, redPacketPos[num2].y + 3f, 30f, 51f), string.Empty, ui_fanli_kelingqu);
				}
				if (btn_fanli_hover.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(redPacketPos[num2].x - 4f, redPacketPos[num2].y - 9f, 56f, 73f), string.Empty) && Global.character_.isAfterAnniersary() != 0)
				{
					cs_festival_reward_get(item2.day);
				}
			}
			num2++;
		}
		num3 = 0;
		if (Global.character_.isInAnniersary() > 0)
		{
			num3 = Global.character_.isInAnniersary();
			if (!flag)
			{
				int num5 = (int)(MainUI.get_Last_RefreshTime(TimeUtil.Now) - (num3 + 18000 - 1296000)) / 86400;
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(redPacketPos[num2].x, redPacketPos[num2].y, 48f, 63f), string.Empty, ui_zhounian_hongbao);
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(redPacketPos[num2].x + 17f, redPacketPos[num2].y + 2f, 13f, 52f), string.Empty, ui_fanli_leijizhong);
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(redPacketPos[num2].x - 4f, redPacketPos[num2].y - 9f, 56f, 73f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "storing"), ui_fanli_hover, GUI.color);
			}
		}
	}

	public bool GetOpen(uint id)
	{
		int targetState = GetTargetState(id);
		bool flag = false;
		if (targetState != 2)
		{
			flag = true;
		}
		return Global.is_premium_new() && flag;
	}

	public bool checkOneTargetAward()
	{
		_register_award register_award = Global.getTemplate("register_award", 1u) as _register_award;
		int sevendaysGot = Global.character_.getSevendaysGot(1u);
		for (int i = 0; i < register_award.award_num; i++)
		{
			_register_award_template register_award_template = Global.getTemplate("register_award_template", (uint)(register_award.award_start_id + i)) as _register_award_template;
			if (Global.character_.level >= register_award_template.requirement && i + 1 > sevendaysGot)
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public void DrawTime(uint id, Rect rt)
	{
		uint register_time = Global.character_.register_time;
		_register_award register_award = Global.getTemplate("register_award", id) as _register_award;
		uint num = MainUI.get_Last_RefreshTime(register_time) + (uint)((register_award.startday - 1) * 60 * 60 * 24);
		uint num2 = (uint)((int)MainUI.get_Last_RefreshTime(register_time) + (register_award.startday - 1) * 60 * 60 * 24 + register_award.days * 60 * 60 * 24);
		int num3 = (int)(num2 - TimeUtil.Now);
		int num4 = 0;
		int num5 = 0;
		DateTime dateTime = new DateTime(1970, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc).AddSeconds(num).ToLocalTime();
		DateTime dateTime2 = new DateTime(1970, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc).AddSeconds(num2).ToLocalTime();
		if (num3 > 86400)
		{
			num4 = num3 / 86400;
			num5 = (num3 - 86400 * num4) / 3600;
			if (register_award.days <= 1)
			{
				if (Global.character_.sevendaysopen[(int)(id - 1)])
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(rt, __BaseUI.load_str(21444u, dateTime.Month, dateTime.Day, num4, num5), __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite, 13);
				}
				else
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(rt, __BaseUI.load_str(21445u, dateTime.Month, dateTime.Day) + "(" + __BaseUI.load_str(9012u) + ")", __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite, 13);
				}
			}
			else if (Global.character_.sevendaysopen[(int)(id - 1)])
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(rt, __BaseUI.load_str(21436u, dateTime.Month, dateTime.Day, dateTime2.Month, dateTime2.Day, num4, num5), __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite, 13);
			}
			else
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(rt, __BaseUI.load_str(21437u, dateTime.Month, dateTime.Day, dateTime2.Month, dateTime2.Day) + "(" + __BaseUI.load_str(9012u) + ")", __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite, 13);
			}
		}
		else if (register_award.days <= 1)
		{
			if (Global.character_.sevendaysopen[(int)(id - 1)])
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(rt, __BaseUI.load_str(21445u, dateTime.Month, dateTime.Day) + "(" + Global.getStringTime(num3) + ")", __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite, 13);
			}
			else
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(rt, __BaseUI.load_str(21445u, dateTime.Month, dateTime.Day, dateTime2.Month, dateTime2.Day) + "(" + __BaseUI.load_str(9012u) + ")", __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite, 13);
			}
		}
		else if (Global.character_.sevendaysopen[(int)(id - 1)])
		{
			__BaseUI.Label(rt, __BaseUI.load_str(21437u, dateTime.Month, dateTime.Day, dateTime2.Month, dateTime2.Day) + "(" + Global.getStringTime(num3) + ")", __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite, 13);
		}
		else
		{
			__BaseUI.Label(rt, __BaseUI.load_str(21437u, dateTime.Month, dateTime.Day, dateTime2.Month, dateTime2.Day) + "(" + __BaseUI.load_str(9012u) + ")", __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite, 13);
		}
	}

	public int GetTargetState(uint id)
	{
		uint register_time = Global.character_.register_time;
		_register_award register_award = Global.getTemplate("register_award", id) as _register_award;
		uint num = MainUI.get_Last_RefreshTime(register_time) + (uint)((register_award.startday - 1) * 60 * 60 * 24);
		uint num2 = (uint)((int)MainUI.get_Last_RefreshTime(register_time) + (register_award.startday - 1) * 60 * 60 * 24 + register_award.days * 60 * 60 * 24);
		if (TimeUtil.Now < num)
		{
			return 1;
		}
		if (TimeUtil.Now > num2)
		{
			return 2;
		}
		return 3;
	}

	private void DrawOneTarget()
	{
		_register_award register_award = Global.getTemplate("register_award", 1u) as _register_award;
		int num = Math.Max(6, register_award.award_num) * 40;
		scrollPos_Onetarget = ScrollControl.infos[scroll_index_Onetarget].scrollpos;
		scrollPos_Onetarget = GUIHacks.BeginScrollView(__BaseUI._rect(420f, 115f, 340f, 240f), scrollPos_Onetarget, __BaseUI._rect(0f, 0f, 340f, num), useHorizontal: false, useVertical: true, new GUIStyle(), __BaseUI._vertScroll1[0], 40, 0, scroll_index_Onetarget);
		ScrollControl.infos[scroll_index_Onetarget].scrollpos = scrollPos_Onetarget;
		for (int i = 0; i < register_award.award_num; i++)
		{
			_register_award_template register_award_template = Global.getTemplate("register_award_template", (uint)(register_award.award_start_id + i)) as _register_award_template;
			__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(0f, 0 + i * 40, 302f, 35f), ui_bar28);
			__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(14f, 2 + i * 40, 77f, 17f), targetlevel);
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(41f, 1 + i * 40, 26f, 20f), register_award_template.requirement.ToString(), __BaseUI._numRight, __BaseUI._clrWhite, 13);
			if (Global.character_.level >= register_award_template.requirement)
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(14f, 18 + i * 40, 200f, 20f), __BaseUI.load_str(21435u), __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrYellow, 12);
			}
			else
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(14f, 18 + i * 40, 200f, 20f), __BaseUI.load_str(21430u, Global.character_.level, register_award_template.requirement), __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrRed, 12);
			}
			__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(121f, 5 + i * 40, 23f, 23f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "id:" + register_award_template.treasure_type + "|" + register_award_template.param1), GetIcon((int)register_award_template.treasure_type));
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(151f, 10 + i * 40, 100f, 20f), __BaseUI.load_str(21069u, register_award_template.param1), __BaseUI._numLeft, __BaseUI._clrCyan, 13);
			int sevendaysGot = Global.character_.getSevendaysGot(1u);
			if (sevendaysGot != 0 && i + 1 <= sevendaysGot)
			{
				__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(212f, 0 + i * 40, 90f, 35f), finishget);
				__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(0f, -1 + i * 40, 302f, 36f), finishbar);
			}
			else if (Global.character_.level >= register_award_template.requirement)
			{
				if (i == sevendaysGot && btnlingqu.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(212f, 6 + i * 40, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
				{
					cs_premium_seven_days_award(1u, (uint)register_award_template.id);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(212f, 0 + i * 40, 77f, 40f), unfinished);
			}
		}
		GUIHacks.EndScrollView();
		DrawTime(1u, new Rect(523f, 413f, 300f, 20f));
	}

	private int GetEquipmetcount()
	{
		int num = 0;
		foreach (KeyValuePair<byte, Equipment> equipment in Global.character_.equipments)
		{
			if (equipment.Value.enchant_level >= 30)
			{
				num++;
			}
		}
		return num;
	}

	private int GetAmuletCount()
	{
		int num = 0;
		foreach (KeyValuePair<byte, Amulet> amulet in Global.character_.amulets)
		{
			if (Global.getTemplate("amulet_template", amulet.Value.idamulet_template) is _amulet_template { quality: >=4 } && amulet.Value.slot != 254 && amulet.Value.slot != byte.MaxValue)
			{
				num++;
			}
		}
		foreach (KeyValuePair<ushort, Gem> gem in Global.character_.gems)
		{
			if (gem.Value.quality >= 4 && gem.Value.slot != byte.MaxValue && gem.Value.slot != 254)
			{
				num++;
			}
		}
		return num;
	}

	private void makecampaignid()
	{
		CampaignId.Clear();
		List<Database.Campaign> list = new List<Database.Campaign>();
		Hashtable templates = Global.getTemplates("campaign");
		_campaign campaign = null;
		foreach (DictionaryEntry item in templates)
		{
			_campaign campaign2 = item.Value as _campaign;
			if (Global.getTemplate("campaign_type", campaign2.idcampaign_type) is _campaign_type { chapter: not false } && campaign2.start)
			{
				campaign = campaign2;
				break;
			}
		}
		list.Add(Global.character_.getCampaign(campaign.id));
		for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
		{
			Database.Campaign campaign3 = list[i];
			foreach (ushort next_campaign in campaign3.m_next_campaigns)
			{
				Database.Campaign campaign4 = Global.character_.getCampaign(next_campaign);
				if (campaign4 != null && !list.Contains(campaign4))
				{
					list.Add(campaign4);
				}
			}
		}
		campaign_cur = -1;
		int num = 0;
		int num2 = 0;
		foreach (Database.Campaign item2 in list)
		{
			if (item2.idcampaign_type != 2 || !item2.unlocked)
			{
				continue;
			}
			item2.tmp_first = item2.first;
			CampaignId.Add((int)item2.idcampaign);
			item2.real_unlocked = true;
			if (!item2.finished && campaign_cur == -1)
			{
				campaign_cur = num2;
			}
			if (campaign_cur != -1)
			{
				num++;
				if (num >= 5)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			num2++;
		}
	}

	private Texture2D GetIcon(int treid)
	{
		Texture2D result = null;
		switch (treid)
		{
		case 4:
			result = __BaseUI._iconMoney;
			break;
		case 5:
			result = __BaseUI._iconMana;
			break;
		case 6:
			result = __BaseUI._iconPractice;
			break;
		case 8:
			result = __BaseUI._tex2Dexp;
			break;
		case 10:
			result = __BaseUI._iconEnchant;
			break;
		case 9:
			result = __BaseUI._iconFruit;
			break;
		case 51:
			result = __BaseUI._icondragonball4;
			break;
		case 55:
			result = __BaseUI._iconjinengsuipian;
			break;
		case 46:
			result = __BaseUI._iconGemEssence;
			break;
		case 18:
			result = __BaseUI._iconReputation;
			break;
		case 39:
			result = __BaseUI.icon_purplesoul;
			break;
		case 40:
			result = __BaseUI.icon_orangesoul;
			break;
		case 30:
			result = __BaseUI._iconStone;
			break;
		case 45:
			result = __BaseUI._iconGemShard;
			break;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public void cs_premium_seven_days_award(uint idx, uint templateid)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSPremiumSevenDaysAward_0x393 cSPremiumSevenDaysAward_0x = new CSPremiumSevenDaysAward_0x393();
			cSPremiumSevenDaysAward_0x.idx = idx;
			cSPremiumSevenDaysAward_0x.templateid = templateid;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(915, cSPremiumSevenDaysAward_0x, sc_premium_seven_days_award, idx);
		}
	}

	public void sc_premium_seven_days_award(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		uint num = (uint)arg;
		SCPremiumSevenDaysAward_0x393 sCPremiumSevenDaysAward_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCPremiumSevenDaysAward_0x393>(stream);
		if (sCPremiumSevenDaysAward_0x.result != 1)
		{
			return;
		}
		List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
		foreach (Treasure bonuse in sCPremiumSevenDaysAward_0x.bonuses)
		{
			_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
			treasure.make(bonuse);
			list.Add(treasure);
		}
		if (list.Count > 0)
		{
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, EnableAll);
			battleResultUI.PrepareLoading("nocollecting");
			battleResultUI.StartLoading();
		}
		if (sCPremiumSevenDaysAward_0x.state != null)
		{
			Global.character_.sevendays[sCPremiumSevenDaysAward_0x.state.days] = new Sevenday();
			Global.character_.sevendays[sCPremiumSevenDaysAward_0x.state.days].Set(sCPremiumSevenDaysAward_0x.state);
		}
	}

	private DateTime getDateTime(double time)
	{
		return new DateTime(1970, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc).AddSeconds(time).ToLocalTime();
	}

	public void updateRewardCount()
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++)
		{
			if (Global.character_.recharge_activity.gbonus[i] == 1)
			{
				Global.character_.recharge_activity_haveReward = 1;
				return;
			}
		}
		for (int j = 0; j < 6; j++)
		{
			if (Global.character_.recharge_activity.ibonus[j] == 1)
			{
				Global.character_.recharge_activity_haveReward = 1;
				return;
			}
		}
		if (Global.character_.recharge_activity.cangetcount > 0)
		{
			Global.character_.recharge_activity_haveReward = 1;
		}
		else
		{
			Global.character_.recharge_activity_haveReward = 0;
		}
	}

	private bool checkXinChunTeHui()
	{
		foreach (KeyValuePair<ushort, FormGeneral> item in Global.character_.form_generals_)
		{
			if (item.Value.idgeneral_template == 282)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		foreach (GeneralsSimple item2 in Global.character_.GeneralsOutSquad)
		{
			if (item2.idtemplate == 282)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		if (Global.getTemplate("sp_general_event_setting", 1u) is _sp_general_event_setting sp_general_event_setting && TimeUtil.Now >= sp_general_event_setting.start_timestamp && TimeUtil.Now <= sp_general_event_setting.end_timestamp)
		{
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	private bool checkHuangCaiMiaoSha()
	{
		if (Global.getTemplate("lottery_event_setting", 1u) is _lottery_event_setting lottery_event_setting && TimeUtil.Now >= lottery_event_setting.start_timestamp && TimeUtil.Now <= lottery_event_setting.end_timestamp && !Global.character_.got_huangcai)
		{
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public bool checkSevenDaysLogin()
	{
		return Global.character_.isSevenDaysLoginOpen && !Global.character_.sevendayslogin.isFinish();
	}

	public bool checkSevenDaysLoginAward()
	{
		return Global.character_.sevendayslogin.canGetBonus();
	}

	public bool checkZhaoZiLongNew()
	{
		if (Global.character_.server_open_time >= 1407513600 && !Global.isNewerZhaoYunQuest)
		{
			if (Global.character_.quest69 >= 5)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public bool checkZhaoZiLongNewAward()
	{
		return Global.character_.quest69 == 4 || Global.character_.VIPLEVEL >= 7;
	}

	public bool checkFund()
	{
		if (checkFundBuyOpen())
		{
			return true;
		}
		foreach (Fund value in Global.character_.funds.Values)
		{
			if (Global.getTemplate("fund_template", value.idfund) is _fund_template fund_template && value.got_return < fund_template.return_day)
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public bool checkFundAward()
	{
		foreach (Fund value in Global.character_.funds.Values)
		{
			if (value.calcFundDays() > value.got_return && value.got_return < value.calcMaxFundReturnDays())
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	{

	}

	public void updatelotty()
	{
		if (lotting)
		{
			if (countdown > 0.0)
			{
				double num = 6.0 / countdown * 15.0;
				if (num > 15.0)
				{
					num = 15.0;
				}
				countdown -= num * (double)Time.deltaTime;
				if (countdown > 0.0)
				{
					curEventType = (int)(9.0 - countdown) % 3;
				}
				else
				{
					curEventType = 0;
				}
				return;
			}
			lottime += Time.deltaTime;
			double num2 = 15.0 - dacc * lottime;
			if (num2 < 0.0)
			{
				double num3 = 112.5 / dacc;
				curEventType = (int)num3 % 3;
				lotting = false;
				lotend = true;
				countdown = 9.0;
			}
			else
			{
				double num4 = (15.0 + num2) / 2.0 * lottime;
				curEventType = (int)num4 % 3;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			curEventType = Global.character_.sp_general_event_type - 1;
		}
	}

	public void sc_get_random_sp_general_event_type_0x704(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			sended = false;
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCGetRandomSpGeneralEventType_0x704 sCGetRandomSpGeneralEventType_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCGetRandomSpGeneralEventType_0x704>(stream);
		if ((long)sCGetRandomSpGeneralEventType_0x.operate == 0L)
		{
			Global.character_.sp_general_event_type = (byte)sCGetRandomSpGeneralEventType_0x.event_type;
			targetEventtype = (int)(sCGetRandomSpGeneralEventType_0x.event_type - 1);
			lottystep = rand.NextDouble() + (double)((rand.Next(0, 1) + 4) * 3) + (double)targetEventtype;
			dacc = 112.5 / lottystep;
			curEventType = 0;
			lottime = 0.0;
			lotting = true;
		}
	}

	public void cs_buy_huangyueying_0x705()

		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;

	public void sc_buy_huangyueying_0x705(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			sended = false;
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCBuyHuangyueying_0x705 sCBuyHuangyueying_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCBuyHuangyueying_0x705>(stream);
		if ((long)sCBuyHuangyueying_0x.operate == 0L)
		{
			Global.character_.gold -= (int)sCBuyHuangyueying_0x.useGold;
			if (sCBuyHuangyueying_0x.useGold != 0)
			{
				RootBase._drawStateBubble("gold-" + sCBuyHuangyueying_0x.useGold);

			{
				BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpecialGetUI>().ShowSPGet_General(sp_general_event_setting.generalid);
			}
		}
	}

	private void DrawHuangCaiMiaoSha()
	{
		Rect rect = new Rect(1f, 0f, -1f, 1f);

		{
			switch (Global.character_.huangcai_event_type)
			{
			case 0:
				drawHuangCaiChouquZi();
				drawHuangCaiDetail();
				if (Global.getTemplate("lottery_event_setting", 1u) is _lottery_event_setting lottery_event_setting && button_hcchouqujl.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(238f, 367f, 247f, 68f), string.Empty) && Level40())
				{
					if (TimeUtil.Now < lottery_event_setting.lottery_startTime)
					{
						__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(9101u, Global.getFormatDate(lottery_event_setting.lottery_startTime) + Global.getFormatDateTime(lottery_event_setting.lottery_startTime)), rmg);
					}
					else
					{
						cs_get_random_huangcai_event_type_0x714();
					}
				}
				break;
			case 1:
				drawHuangCaiGoumaiZi();
				drawHuangCaiDetail();
				drawHuangCaiBuyBtn();
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(311f, 220f, 101f, 132f), string.Empty, ui_hcka_hover);
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(199f, 200f, 101f, 132f), string.Empty, ui_hcka_mengban);
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(423f, 241f, 101f, 132f), string.Empty, ui_hcka_mengban);
				break;
			case 2:
				drawHuangCaiGoumaiZi();
				drawHuangCaiDetail();
				drawHuangCaiBuyBtn();
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(311f, 220f, 101f, 132f), string.Empty, ui_hcka_mengban);
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(199f, 200f, 101f, 132f), string.Empty, ui_hcka_hover);
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(423f, 241f, 101f, 132f), string.Empty, ui_hcka_mengban);
				break;
			case 3:
				drawHuangCaiGoumaiZi();
				drawHuangCaiDetail();
				drawHuangCaiBuyBtn();
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(311f, 220f, 101f, 132f), string.Empty, ui_hcka_mengban);
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(199f, 200f, 101f, 132f), string.Empty, ui_hcka_mengban);
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(423f, 241f, 101f, 132f), string.Empty, ui_hcka_hover);
				break;
			case 4:
				drawHuangCaiGoumaiZi();
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(185f, 185f, 355f, 182f), string.Empty, ui_hc_guoqi);
				drawHuangCaiBuyBtn();
				break;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			drawHuangCaiDetail();
			switch (curEventType)
			{
			case 0:
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(311f, 220f, 101f, 132f), string.Empty, ui_hcka_hover);
				break;
			case 1:
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(199f, 200f, 101f, 132f), string.Empty, ui_hcka_hover);
				break;
			case 2:
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(423f, 241f, 101f, 132f), string.Empty, ui_hcka_hover);
				break;
			}
		}
		GUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(519f, 206f, 90f, 89f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "huangcai"), __BaseUI.normalStyle);
	}

	private void drawHuangCaiBuyBtn()
	{
		if (Global.character_.got_huangcai)
		{
			return;
		}
		int num = 0;
		foreach (object value in Global.getTemplates("lottery_event_type_chance").Values)
		{
			_lottery_event_type_chance lottery_event_type_chance = value as _lottery_event_type_chance;
			if (lottery_event_type_chance.id_event == 1 && lottery_event_type_chance.event_type == Global.character_.huangcai_event_type)
			{
				num = (int)lottery_event_type_chance.cost_treasure_param1;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (num > 0)
		{
			if (button_hclijigoumai.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(238f, 367f, 247f, 68f), string.Empty) && Level40())
			{
				__BaseUI.GetConsumeUI().setConsume(num, 98, HuangCaiGoldOK);
			}
		}
		else if (num == 0 && button_hclingqujl.Button(bBtn: true, __BaseUI._rect(238f, 367f, 247f, 68f), string.Empty) && Level40())
		{
			cs_buy_huangcai_0x715();
		}
	}

	public void sc_buy_huangcai_0x715(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			sended = false;
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCBuyHuangCai_0x715 sCBuyHuangCai_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCBuyHuangCai_0x715>(stream);
		if ((long)sCBuyHuangCai_0x.operate == 0L)
		{
			foreach (Treasure item in sCBuyHuangCai_0x.resource_consume)
			{
				Global.character_.remParam(item.idtreasure_type, item.param0, item.param1);
				if (item.idtreasure_type == 7)
				{
					__BaseUI.drawStateBubble("gold-" + item.param1);
				}
			}
			List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
			foreach (Treasure bonu in sCBuyHuangCai_0x.bonus)
			{
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make(bonu);
				list.Add(treasure);
			}
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, ResumeUI);
			battleResultUI.MyLoading();
			Global.character_.got_huangcai = true;
		}
		else if (sCBuyHuangCai_0x.operate == SCBuyHuangCai_0x715.Operate.Mailed)
		{
			__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(21593u), rmg);
			Global.character_.got_huangcai = true;
		}
	}

	private bool Level40()
	{
		if (Global.character_.level < 40)
		{
			__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(9205u), rmg);
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	private void DrawSevenDaysLogin()
	{
		if (Global.character_.sevendayslogin == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		bool flag = false;
		for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++)
		{
			Rect rc = __BaseUI._rect(187 + 83 * i, 313f, 80f, 88f);
			__BaseUI.Label(rc, string.Empty, ui_denglu[i]);
			int day = i + 1;
			if (!Global.character_.sevendayslogin.hasGotLoginBonus(day))
			{
				if (btn_bonusget.Button(IsEnable() && Global.character_.sevendayslogin.canGetBonus(day), __BaseUI._rect(rc.x + 7f, 390f, 66f, 30f), string.Empty))
				{
					if (Global.isNewNewbieActivity)
					{
						cs_seven_days_login_bonus_get_new_0x721((uint)day);
					}
					else
					{

				}
			}
			else
			{

			}
			if (GUITOScreen(rc).Contains(new Vector2(Input.mousePosition.x, (float)Screen.height - Input.mousePosition.y)))
			{
				if (i != 2)
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(rc, string.Empty, ui_denglu_hover);
				}
				else
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(rc, string.Empty, ui_denglu3_hover);

				showBonusDay = day;

				return;

	public void cs_seven_days_login_bonus_get_0x71A(uint _day)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSSevenDaysLoginBonusGet_0x71A cSSevenDaysLoginBonusGet_0x71A = new CSSevenDaysLoginBonusGet_0x71A();
			cSSevenDaysLoginBonusGet_0x71A.day = _day;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(1818, cSSevenDaysLoginBonusGet_0x71A, sc_seven_days_login_bonus_get_0x71A);
		}
	}

	public void sc_seven_days_login_bonus_get_0x71A(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			sended = false;
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCSevenDaysLoginBonusGet_0x71A sCSevenDaysLoginBonusGet_0x71A = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCSevenDaysLoginBonusGet_0x71A>(stream);
		if ((long)sCSevenDaysLoginBonusGet_0x71A.operate != 0L)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (sCSevenDaysLoginBonusGet_0x71A.sevendays_login != null)
		{
			Global.character_.sevendayslogin.Set(sCSevenDaysLoginBonusGet_0x71A.sevendays_login);
		}
		List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
		foreach (Treasure bonu in sCSevenDaysLoginBonusGet_0x71A.bonus)
		{
			if (bonu.idtreasure_type == 0)
			{
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make(bonu);
				BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpecialGetUI>().ShowSPGet(treasure);
			}
			else
			{
				_Treasure treasure2 = new _Treasure();
				treasure2.make(bonu);
				list.Add(treasure2);
			}
		}
		BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
		battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, ResumeUI);
		battleResultUI.MyLoading();
	}

	private void DrawZhaoZiLongNew()
	{
		Rect rc = new Rect(511f, 68f, 259f, 383f);
		if (GUITOScreen(rc).Contains(new Vector2(Input.mousePosition.x, (float)Screen.height - Input.mousePosition.y)) && GUI.Button(__BaseUI._rect(424f, 68f, 349f, 383f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "generalR234"), zilongHover))
		{
			seeGenDetail(234u);
		}
		if (Global.character_.VIPLEVEL < 7 && Global.character_.quest69 < 4 && chargeBtn.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(257f, 218f, 217f, 49f), string.Empty))
		{
			RootUI.Coin();
		}
		if (!MainUI.checkUI("army_recruit"))
		{
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(214f, 283f, 291f, 50f), string.Empty, ui_bar_4);
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(258f, 300f, 182f, 15f), string.Empty, ui_zi_zm);
		}
		else
		{
			if (!(Global.getTemplate("premium_general_quest", 69u) is _premium_general_quest premium_general_quest))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (Global.character_.quest69 == 4 || Global.character_.VIPLEVEL >= 7)
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(233f, 249f, 275f, 88f), string.Empty, ui_bar_5);
				if (recruitBtn.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(357f, 297f, 98f, 30f), string.Empty))
				{
					cs_zhaoyun_quest_new(Global.isNewNewbieActivity);
				}
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < questDescs.Length && Global.getTemplate("premium_quest", (uint)(i + premium_general_quest.quest_startid)) is _premium_quest; i++)
			{
				if (i == Global.character_.quest69)
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(214f, 293 + step * i, 291f, 28f), string.Empty, ui_bar_3);
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(240f, 293 + step * i, 50f, 28f), __BaseUI.load_str(20854u) + (i + 1), __BaseUI._textLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite, __BaseUI._fontSize);
					if (questDescs[i] != null)
					{
						questDescs[i].draw(__BaseUI._rect(290f, 299 + step * i, 150f, 28f), __BaseUI._textNLeft);
					}
					if (Global.character_.quest69 == 0)
					{
						if (Global.character_.level >= 70)
						{
							if (ui_lingqu2.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
							{
								cs_zhaoyun_quest_new(Global.isNewNewbieActivity);
							}
						}
						else if (btnGoldPay.Button(Global.character_.VIPLEVEL >= 5, __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "goldzhaozilong")))
						{
							ZhaoyunGoldCostNew();
						}
					}
					else if (Global.character_.quest69 == 1)
					{
						if (doHaveGeneral(69u))
						{
							if (ui_lingqu2.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
							{
								cs_zhaoyun_quest_new(Global.isNewNewbieActivity);
							}
						}
						else if (btnGoldPay.Button(Global.character_.VIPLEVEL >= 5, __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "goldzhaozilong")))
						{
							ZhaoyunGoldCostNew();
						}
					}
					else
					{
						if (Global.character_.quest69 != 2 && Global.character_.quest69 != 3)
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (checkQuestNeed(Global.character_.quest69 + premium_general_quest.quest_startid))
						{
							if (btnPay.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
							{
								cs_zhaoyun_quest_new(Global.isNewNewbieActivity);
							}
						}
						else if (btnGoldPay.Button(Global.character_.VIPLEVEL >= 5, __BaseUI._rect(420f, 296 + step * i, 80f, 22f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "goldzhaozilong")))
						{
							ZhaoyunGoldCostNew();
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(240f, 293 + step * i, 50f, 28f), __BaseUI.load_str(20854u) + (i + 1), __BaseUI._textLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite, __BaseUI._fontSize);
					questDescs[i].draw(__BaseUI._rect(290f, 299 + step * i, 150f, 28f), __BaseUI._textNLeft);
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(214f, 293 + step * i, 291f, 28f), string.Empty, ui_bar_4);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public void cs_zhaoyun_quest_new(bool _isNewNewbieActivity)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSZhaoyunQuestNew_0x3B4 cSZhaoyunQuestNew_0x3B = new CSZhaoyunQuestNew_0x3B4();
			cSZhaoyunQuestNew_0x3B.isNewNewbieActivity = _isNewNewbieActivity;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(948, cSZhaoyunQuestNew_0x3B, sc_zhaoyun_quest_new);
		}
	}

	public void sc_zhaoyun_quest_new(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCZhaoyunQuestNew_0x3B4 sCZhaoyunQuestNew_0x3B = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCZhaoyunQuestNew_0x3B4>(stream);
		switch (sCZhaoyunQuestNew_0x3B.operate)
		{
		case SCZhaoyunQuestNew_0x3B4.Operate.OK:
		{
			Global.character_.quest69 = (byte)sCZhaoyunQuestNew_0x3B.step;
			if (Global.character_.VIPLEVEL < 7 && Global.getTemplate("premium_general_quest", 69u) is _premium_general_quest premium_general_quest)
			{
				if (Global.isNewNewbieActivity)
				{
					if (Global.getTemplate("premium_quest_new", (uint)(Global.character_.quest69 - 1 + premium_general_quest.quest_startid)) is _premium_quest_new premium_quest_new)
					{
						_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
						treasure.make((byte)premium_quest_new.need_treasure, 0, premium_quest_new.need_amount);
						Global.character_.remParam(treasure.name, treasure.param[1]);
					}
				}
				else if (Global.getTemplate("premium_quest", (uint)(Global.character_.quest69 - 1 + premium_general_quest.quest_startid)) is _premium_quest premium_quest)
				{
					_Treasure treasure2 = new _Treasure();
					treasure2.make((byte)premium_quest.need_treasure, 0, premium_quest.need_amount);
					Global.character_.remParam(treasure2.name, treasure2.param[1]);
				}
			}
			List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
			foreach (Treasure bonuse in sCZhaoyunQuestNew_0x3B.bonuses)
			{
				if (bonuse.idtreasure_type == 0 || bonuse.idtreasure_type == 36)
				{
					_Treasure treasure3 = new _Treasure();
					treasure3.make(bonuse);
					BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpecialGetUI>().ShowSPGet(treasure3);
				}
				else
				{
					_Treasure treasure4 = new _Treasure();
					treasure4.make(bonuse);
					list.Add(treasure4);
				}
			}
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, EnableAll);
			battleResultUI.MyLoading();
			break;
		}
		}
	}

	private void ZhaoyunGoldCostNew()
	{
		if (!(Global.getTemplate("premium_general_quest", 69u) is _premium_general_quest premium_general_quest))
		{
			return;
		}
		if (Global.isNewNewbieActivity)
		{
			if (Global.getTemplate("premium_quest_new", (uint)(Global.character_.quest69 + premium_general_quest.quest_startid)) is _premium_quest_new premium_quest_new)
			{
				__BaseUI.GetConsumeUI().setConsume(premium_quest_new.gold_cost, 54, ZhaoyunGoldOKNew, new object[1] { Global.isNewNewbieActivity });
			}
		}
		else if (Global.getTemplate("premium_quest", (uint)(Global.character_.quest69 + premium_general_quest.quest_startid)) is _premium_quest premium_quest)
		{
			__BaseUI.GetConsumeUI().setConsume(premium_quest.gold_cost, 54, ZhaoyunGoldOKNew, new object[1] { Global.isNewNewbieActivity });
		}
	}

		if (bOK && arg != null)

		}
	}
