				if (Global.isNewNewbieActivity)
				{
					if (Global.getTemplate("premium_quest_new", (uint)(Global.character_.quest69 - 1 + premium_general_quest.quest_startid)) is _premium_quest_new premium_quest_new)
					{
						Global.character_.gold -= premium_quest_new.gold_cost;
						__BaseUI.drawStateBubble("gold-" + premium_quest_new.gold_cost);
					}
				}
				else if (Global.getTemplate("premium_quest", (uint)(Global.character_.quest69 - 1 + premium_general_quest.quest_startid)) is _premium_quest premium_quest)
				{
					Global.character_.gold -= premium_quest.gold_cost;
					__BaseUI.drawStateBubble("gold-" + premium_quest.gold_cost);
				}
			}
			List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
			foreach (Treasure bonuse in sCZhaoyunQuestGoldNew_0x3B.bonuses)
			{
				if (bonuse.idtreasure_type == 0 || bonuse.idtreasure_type == 36)
				{
					_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
					treasure.make(bonuse);
					BaseUI.GetRootUI().GetComponent<SpecialGetUI>().ShowSPGet(treasure);
				}
				else
				{
					_Treasure treasure2 = new _Treasure();
					treasure2.make(bonuse);
					list.Add(treasure2);
				}
			}
			BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
			battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, EnableAll);
			battleResultUI.MyLoading();
			break;
		}
		}
	}

	private void DrawFund()
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
		{
			ushort num = (ushort)(i + 1);
			if (isHideFundContent(num))
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(353f, 143 + 152 * i, 399f, 117f), string.Empty, ui_mengban);
				continue;
			}
			Rect position = __BaseUI._rect(451f, 147 + 152 * i, 300f, 112f);
			ref Vector2 reference = ref scroll_fund[i];
			reference = ScrollControl.infos[scroll_index_fund[i]].scrollpos;
			ref Vector2 reference2 = ref scroll_fund[i];
			reference2 = GUIHacks.BeginScrollView(position, scroll_fund[i], contrect[i], useHorizontal: false, useVertical: true, new GUIStyle(), __BaseUI._vertScroll1[0], fundStep, 0, scroll_index_fund[i]);
			ScrollControl.infos[scroll_index_fund[i]].scrollpos = scroll_fund[i];
			__BaseUI.Label(contrect[i], string.Empty, ui_xian[i]);
			dicFundReturnIdx.Clear();
			if (Global.is_premium_fund_new())
			{
				Hashtable templates = Global.getTemplates("fund_return");
				foreach (object value in templates.Values)
				{
					if (!(value is _fund_return fund_return) || fund_return.idfund != num)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (!dicFundReturnIdx.ContainsKey(num))
					{
						dicFundReturnIdx[num] = new Dictionary<int, int>();
					}
					if (!dicFundReturnIdx[num].ContainsKey(fund_return.day))
					{
						dicFundReturnIdx[num][fund_return.day] = 0;
					}
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(70 + 72 * dicFundReturnIdx[num][fund_return.day], 3 + 29 * (fund_return.day - 1), 23f, 23f), string.Empty, Global.getBonusSmallIcon((ushort)fund_return.treasure_type, fund_return.param0));
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(95 + 72 * dicFundReturnIdx[num][fund_return.day], 4 + 29 * (fund_return.day - 1), 50f, 20f), Global.getFormatNumber((uint)fund_return.param1), __BaseUI._textLeft, __BaseUI._clrYellowLight, __BaseUI._fontSize);
					dicFundReturnIdx[num][fund_return.day] = dicFundReturnIdx[num][fund_return.day] + 1;
					if (Global.character_.funds.ContainsKey(fund_return.idfund))
					{
						if (Global.character_.funds[fund_return.idfund].got_return >= fund_return.day)
						{
							__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(234f, 1 + 29 * (fund_return.day - 1), 27f, 26f), string.Empty, ui_gou);
						}
						else if (Global.character_.funds[fund_return.idfund].calcFundDays() >= fund_return.day)
						{
							__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(234f, 5 + 29 * (fund_return.day - 1), 31f, 18f), string.Empty, ui_keling);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				Hashtable templates2 = Global.getTemplates("fund_return_old");
				foreach (object value2 in templates2.Values)
				{
					if (!(value2 is _fund_return_old fund_return_old) || fund_return_old.idfund != num)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (!dicFundReturnIdx.ContainsKey(num))
					{
						dicFundReturnIdx[num] = new Dictionary<int, int>();
					}
					if (!dicFundReturnIdx[num].ContainsKey(fund_return_old.day))
					{
						dicFundReturnIdx[num][fund_return_old.day] = 0;
					}
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(70 + 72 * dicFundReturnIdx[num][fund_return_old.day], 3 + 29 * (fund_return_old.day - 1), 23f, 23f), string.Empty, Global.getBonusSmallIcon((ushort)fund_return_old.treasure_type, fund_return_old.param0));
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(95 + 72 * dicFundReturnIdx[num][fund_return_old.day], 4 + 29 * (fund_return_old.day - 1), 50f, 20f), Global.getFormatNumber((uint)fund_return_old.param1), __BaseUI._textLeft, __BaseUI._clrYellowLight, __BaseUI._fontSize);
					dicFundReturnIdx[num][fund_return_old.day] = dicFundReturnIdx[num][fund_return_old.day] + 1;
					if (Global.character_.funds.ContainsKey(fund_return_old.idfund))
					{
						if (Global.character_.funds[fund_return_old.idfund].got_return >= fund_return_old.day)
						{
							__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(234f, 1 + 29 * (fund_return_old.day - 1), 27f, 26f), string.Empty, ui_gou);
						}
						else if (Global.character_.funds[fund_return_old.idfund].calcFundDays() >= fund_return_old.day)
						{
							__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(234f, 5 + 29 * (fund_return_old.day - 1), 31f, 18f), string.Empty, ui_keling);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			GUIHacks.EndScrollView();
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(450f, 261 + 152 * i, 205f, 37f), string.Empty, ui_guang);
			if (Global.character_.funds.ContainsKey(num))
			{
				if (btn_getfundreturn.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(512f, 268 + 152 * i, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
				{
					if (Global.character_.funds[num].calcFundDays() > Global.character_.funds[num].got_return && Global.character_.funds[num].got_return < Global.character_.funds[num].calcMaxFundReturnDays())
					{
						cs_fund_return_get(num, Global.is_premium_fund_new());
					}
					else if (Global.character_.funds[num].got_return == Global.character_.funds[num].calcMaxFundReturnDays())
					{
						__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(9220u), rmg);
					}
					else
					{
						__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(9215u), rmg);
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (!btn_fundbuy.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(512f, 268 + 152 * i, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (checkFundBuyOpen())
				{
					if (Global.getTemplate("fund_template", num) is _fund_template fund_template)
					{
						__BaseUI.GetConsumeUI().setConsume(fund_template.cost, 104, FundBuyOK, new object[1] { (uint)num });
					}
				}
				else
				{
					__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(9216u), rmg);
				}
			}
		}
		uint num2 = (uint)((int)(TimeUtil.ThisDay + 18000) - MainUI.CalcServerOpenRefreshDaysTest() * 86400 + 604800);
		if (TimeUtil.Now < Global.fund_setting.end_time)
		{
			num2 = Math.Max(num2, Global.fund_setting.end_time);
		}
		int t = 0;
		if (TimeUtil.Now < num2)
		{
			t = (int)(num2 - TimeUtil.Now);
		}
		__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(335f, 422f, 150f, 20f), Global.getStringTime(t), __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrGreen, __BaseUI._fontSize);
	}

	public bool isHideFundContent(ushort fundid)
	{
		bool flag = false;
		if (Global.getTemplate("fund_template", fundid) is _fund_template fund_template)
		{
			Hashtable templates = Global.getTemplates("fund_template");
			foreach (object value in templates.Values)
			{
				if (value is _fund_template fund_template2 && fund_template2.id != fund_template.id && fund_template2.idevent == fund_template.idevent && Global.character_.funds.ContainsKey(fund_template2.id))
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (flag && !Global.character_.funds.ContainsKey(fundid))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public bool checkFundBuyOpen()
	{
		if ((TimeUtil.Now >= Global.fund_setting.start_time && TimeUtil.Now <= Global.fund_setting.end_time) || MainUI.CalcServerOpenRefreshDaysTest() < 7)
		{
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public void FundBuyOK(bool bOK, object[] arg)
	{
		if (bOK && arg != null)
		{
			uint fundid = (uint)arg[0];
			cs_fund_buy(fundid);
		}
	}

	public void cs_fund_buy(uint _fundid)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSFundBuy_0x71E cSFundBuy_0x71E = new CSFundBuy_0x71E();
			cSFundBuy_0x71E.fundid = _fundid;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(1822, cSFundBuy_0x71E, sc_fund_buy);
		}
	}

	public void sc_fund_buy(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			sended = false;
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCFundBuy_0x71E sCFundBuy_0x71E = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCFundBuy_0x71E>(stream);
		if ((long)sCFundBuy_0x71E.operate != 0L)
		{
			return;
		}
		Global.character_.gold -= (int)sCFundBuy_0x71E.gold_consume;
		__BaseUI.drawStateBubble("gold-" + sCFundBuy_0x71E.gold_consume);
		Global.character_.funds.Clear();
		foreach (db_character_fund fund2 in sCFundBuy_0x71E.funds)
		{
			Fund fund = new Fund();
			fund.Set(fund2);
			Global.character_.funds.Add(fund.idfund, fund);
		}
	}

	public void cs_fund_return_get(uint _fundid, bool _isPremiumFundNew)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSFundReturnGet_0x71D cSFundReturnGet_0x71D = new CSFundReturnGet_0x71D();
			cSFundReturnGet_0x71D.fundid = _fundid;
			cSFundReturnGet_0x71D.isPremiumFundNew = _isPremiumFundNew;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(1821, cSFundReturnGet_0x71D, sc_fund_return_get);
		}
	}

	public void sc_fund_return_get(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			sended = false;
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCFundReturnGet_0x71D sCFundReturnGet_0x71D = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCFundReturnGet_0x71D>(stream);
		if ((long)sCFundReturnGet_0x71D.operate != 0L)
		{
			return;
		}
		Global.character_.funds.Clear();
		foreach (db_character_fund fund2 in sCFundReturnGet_0x71D.funds)
		{
			Fund fund = new Fund();
			fund.Set(fund2);
			Global.character_.funds.Add(fund.idfund, fund);
		}
		List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
		foreach (Treasure bonu in sCFundReturnGet_0x71D.bonus)
		{
			_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
			treasure.make(bonu);
			list.Add(treasure);
		}
		BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
		battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, ResumeUI);
		battleResultUI.MyLoading();
	}

	public void UpdateOT()
	{
		if (!m_lotting)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (m_countdown > 0.0)
		{
			double num = 24.0 / m_countdown * 36.0;
			if (num > 36.0)
			{
				num = 36.0;
			}
			m_countdown -= num * (double)Time.deltaTime;
			if (m_countdown > 0.0)
			{
				m_cur_highlight = (int)(36.0 - m_countdown) % 12;
			}
			else
			{
				m_cur_highlight = 0;
			}
			return;
		}
		m_lottime += Time.deltaTime;
		double num2 = 36.0 - m_dacc * m_lottime;
		if (num2 < 0.0)
		{
			double num3 = 648.0 / m_dacc;
			m_cur_highlight = (int)num3 % 12;
			m_lotting = false;
			m_lotend = true;
			m_countdown = 36.0;
			if (m_Bonus.Count > 0)
			{
				BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
				battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, m_Bonus, recall);
				battleResultUI.MyLoading();
			}
		}
		else
		{
			double num4 = (36.0 + num2) / 2.0 * m_lottime;
			m_cur_highlight = (int)num4 % 12;
		}
	}

	public bool checkOnlineLottery()
	{
		Hashtable templates = Global.getTemplates("online_lottery_template");
		if (Global.character_.onlinelottery.lottery_today >= templates.Count)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public bool checkOnlineLotteryAward()
	{
		if (Global.character_.onlinelottery.getcdtime() == 0)
		{
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public void DrawOnlineLottery()
	{
		Hashtable templates = Global.getTemplates("online_lottery_template");
		if (Btn_choujiang.Button(m_cur_highlight == -1, __BaseUI._rect(525f, 174f, 141f, 141f), string.Empty))
		{
			if (Global.character_.onlinelottery.lottery_today >= templates.Count)
			{
				__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(21646u), rmg, _return);
			}
			else if (Global.character_.onlinelottery.getcdtime() != 0)
			{
				__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(21647u), rmg);
			}
			else
			{
				cs_online_lottery_draw();
			}
		}
		__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(544f, 84f, 200f, 16f), Global.character_.onlinelottery.lottery_today + "/" + templates.Count, __BaseUI._numLeft, __BaseUI._clrGreen);
		if (Global.character_.onlinelottery.getcdtime() == 0)
		{
			__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(684f, 84f, 200f, 16f), __BaseUI.load_str(21648u), __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrGreen);
		}
		else
		{
			__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(684f, 84f, 200f, 16f), Global.getFormatTime((int)Global.character_.onlinelottery.getcdtime()), __BaseUI._numLeft, __BaseUI._clrGreen);
		}
		int num = 0;
		foreach (OnlineLotteryItem value in Global.character_.onlinelottery.m_onlineLotteryItems.Values)
		{
			if (!(Global.getTemplate("online_lottery_item", (uint)value.iditem) is _online_lottery_item online_lottery_item))
			{
				continue;
			}
			__BaseUI.BeginGroup(__BaseUI._rect(lottery_pos[num].x, lottery_pos[num].y, 56f, 56f));
			m_t.make(online_lottery_item.item_type, online_lottery_item.item_param0, online_lottery_item.item_param1);
			if (m_t.texIcon != null)
			{
				if (m_t.texIcon.name != m_t.getIconName())
				{
					AddAssetSub(m_t.getIconName());
					StartLoadingSub();
				}
				if (m_t.texIcon.name == m_t.getIconName())
				{
					__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(0f, 0f, 56f, 56f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "ol" + online_lottery_item.item_type + "|" + online_lottery_item.item_param1), m_t.texIcon);
					__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(0f, 42f, 56f, 16f), "x" + Global.getFormatNumber((uint)m_t.param[1]), __BaseUI._textNRight, __BaseUI._clrGreen);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				AddAssetSub(m_t.getIconName());
				StartLoadingSub();
			}
			__BaseUI.EndGroup();
			if (m_cur_highlight == num)
			{
				__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(lottery_pos[num].x - 12f, lottery_pos[num].y - 12f, 80f, 80f), ui_zxjl_hover);
			}
			num++;
		}
		if (Global.character_.gm && GUI.Button(__BaseUI._rect(170f, 100f, 100f, 20f), "娓呴櫎鎶藉?娆℃暟"))
		{
			cs_debug_online_lottery();
		}
	}

	public void cs_online_lottery_draw()
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F cSOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F = new CSOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F();
			cSOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F.clientUpdated = true;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(1823, cSOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F, sc_online_lotteyr_draw);
		}
	}

	public void sc_online_lotteyr_draw(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		sended = false;
		SCOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F sCOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F>(stream);
		if (sCOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F.operate == SCOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F.Operate.OK)
		{
			m_ol = sCOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F.lotteryitems;
			m_col = sCOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F.onlinelottery;
			m_Bonus.Clear();
			foreach (Treasure bonu in sCOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F.bonus)
			{
				_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
				treasure.make(bonu);
				m_Bonus.Add(treasure);
			}
			target_lottery = sCOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F.iditem_got;
			int num = 0;
			foreach (OnlineLotteryItem value in Global.character_.onlinelottery.m_onlineLotteryItems.Values)
			{
				if (value.iditem == target_lottery)
				{
					m_step = m_rand.NextDouble() + (double)((m_rand.Next(0, 1) + 6) * 12) + (double)num;
				}
				num++;
			}
			m_dacc = 648.0 / m_step;
			m_cur_highlight = 0;
			m_lottime = 0.0;
			m_lotting = true;
		}
		else if (sCOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F.operate != SCOnlineLotteryDraw_0x71F.Operate.Error)
		{
		}
	}

	private void recall()
	{
		EnableAll();
		if (m_col != null)
		{
			m_cur_highlight = -1;
			Global.character_.onlinelottery.Set(m_col);
			Global.character_.onlinelottery.m_onlineLotteryItems.Clear();
			foreach (db_character_online_lottery_item item in m_ol)
			{
				OnlineLotteryItem onlineLotteryItem = new OnlineLotteryItem();
				onlineLotteryItem.Set(item);
				Global.character_.onlinelottery.m_onlineLotteryItems.Add(onlineLotteryItem.iditem, onlineLotteryItem);
			}
		}
		Hashtable templates = Global.getTemplates("online_lottery_template");
		if (Global.character_.onlinelottery.lottery_today >= templates.Count)
		{
			__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(21646u), rmg, _return);
		}
	}

	private void _return()
	{
		MainUI.SetSubUI(null);
		Hide();
	}

	public void cs_debug_online_lottery()
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSDebugOnlineLotteryTimesClear_0x720 pkt = new CSDebugOnlineLotteryTimesClear_0x720();
			RootUI.m_client.Send(1824, pkt, sc_debug_online_lottery);
		}
	}

	public void sc_debug_online_lottery(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		if (stream != null)
		{
			sended = false;
			SCDebugOnlineLotteryTimesClear_0x720 sCDebugOnlineLotteryTimesClear_0x = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCDebugOnlineLotteryTimesClear_0x720>(stream);
			switch (sCDebugOnlineLotteryTimesClear_0x.operate = SCDebugOnlineLotteryTimesClear_0x720.Operate.OK)
			{
			case SCDebugOnlineLotteryTimesClear_0x720.Operate.OK:
				Global.character_.onlinelottery.lottery_today = 0;
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	private void loadGiftBagAsset(AssetBundle uibundle)
	{
		ui_fudai1.normal.background = uibundle.Load("ui_fudai1") as Texture2D;

		ui_fudai_bar2.normal.background = uibundle.Load("ui_fudai_bar2") as Texture2D;
		for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++)
		{
			ui_dengji[i] = uibundle.Load("ui_dengji" + (i + 1)) as Texture2D;
		}
		ui_dkfd.normal.background = uibundle.Load("ui_dkfd") as Texture2D;
		ui_fkdk.normal.background = uibundle.Load("ui_fkdk") as Texture2D;
		ui_fudai_bar1.normal.background = uibundle.Load("ui_fudai_bar1") as Texture2D;
		button_dkfd.make(uibundle, "button_dkfd");
		ui_fdylw.normal.background = uibundle.Load("ui_fdylw") as Texture2D;
		ui_icon_fudai1 = uibundle.Load("ui_icon_fudai1") as Texture2D;
		ui_icon_fudai2 = uibundle.Load("ui_icon_fudai2") as Texture2D;
		for (int j = 0; j < 10; j++)
		{
			ui_vipzi[j] = uibundle.Load("ui_vipzi" + (j + 1)) as Texture2D;
		}
		Btn_openbag.make(uibundle, "button_dkfd");
		ui_fd_chongzhi = uibundle.Load("ui_fd_chongzhi") as Texture2D;
		InitBag();
	}

	private void InitBag()
	{
		switch (Global.character_.VIPLEVEL)
		{
		case 0:
		case 1:
		case 2:
			baglevel = 1u;
			break;
		case 3:
		case 4:
			baglevel = 2u;
			break;
		case 5:
		case 6:
			baglevel = 3u;
			break;
		case 7:
		case 8:
		case 9:
			baglevel = 4u;
			break;
		case 10:
			baglevel = 5u;
			break;
		}
		m_indexlist.Clear();
		foreach (GiftBag value in Global.character_.giftbags.Values)
		{
			if (!value.got && value.start_time + 604800 > TimeUtil.Now)
			{
				m_indexlist.Add(value.idgift_bag);
			}
		}
		SortIdGift();
		nowpage = 1;
	}

	private void SortIdGift()
	{
		m_indexlist.Sort((uint a, uint b) => a.CompareTo(b));
	}

	public bool checkGiftBag()
	{
		if (Global.isGiftbagOpen)
		{
			foreach (GiftBag value in Global.character_.giftbags.Values)
			{
				if (!value.got && value.start_time + 604800 > TimeUtil.Now)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	private void DrawGiftBag()
	{
		uint num = 1u;
		if (m_indexlist.Count > 0)
		{
			num = m_indexlist[nowpage - 1];
		}
		switch (num)
		{
		case 1u:
			ui_difudai = ui_fudai1;
			break;
		case 2u:
			ui_difudai = ui_fudai2;
			break;
		case 3u:
			ui_difudai = ui_fudai2;
			break;
		case 4u:
			ui_difudai = ui_fudai2;
			break;
		case 5u:
			ui_difudai = ui_fudai3;
			break;
		}
		giftbag giftbag = new giftbag();
		if (!Global.isNewNewbieActivity)
		{
			if (Global.getTemplate("gift_bag_old", num) is _gift_bag_old gift_bag_old)
			{
				giftbag = new giftbag((int)gift_bag_old.id, (int)gift_bag_old.cost, (int)gift_bag_old.required_level, (int)gift_bag_old.required_vip_level);
			}
		}
		else if (Global.getTemplate("gift_bag", num) is _gift_bag gift_bag)
		{
			giftbag = new giftbag((int)gift_bag.id, (int)gift_bag.cost, (int)gift_bag.required_level, (int)gift_bag.required_vip_level);
		}
		if (giftbag == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (!GetAll())
		{
			if (!nowGetall())
			{
				__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(bagtre_pos[num - 1].x + 55f, bagtre_pos[num - 1].y - 25f, 85f, 22f), ui_fdjl);
				if (nowpage > 1 && __BaseUI._btnPageDown5.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(202f, 309f, 48f, 61f), string.Empty))
				{
					nowpage--;
				}
				if (nowpage < m_indexlist.Count && __BaseUI._btnPageUp5.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(473f, 309f, 48f, 61f), string.Empty))
				{
					nowpage++;
				}
				if (ui_difudai != null)
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(230f, 153f, 264f, 199f), string.Empty, ui_difudai);
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(225f, 126f, 57f, 164f), string.Empty, ui_fudai_bar2);
					GUI.DrawTexture(__BaseUI._rect(238f, 150f, 24f, 25f), ui_dengji[num - 1]);
				}
				int num2 = 0;
				if (giftbag.id != m_indexlist[0])
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(263f, 416f, 212f, 23f), string.Empty, ui_kqdj);
				}
				else if (giftbag.required_level > Global.character_.level)
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(263f, 416f, 183f, 24f), string.Empty, ui_fkdk);
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(290f, 419f, 19f, 18f), giftbag.required_level, __BaseUI._numCenter, __BaseUI._clrRed);
				}
				else if (giftbag.cost > 0)
				{
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(263f, 416f, 183f, 24f), string.Empty, ui_dkfd);
					__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(317f, 419f, 45f, 18f), giftbag.cost.ToString(), __BaseUI._numCenter, fontclr);
				}
				if (Btn_openbag.Button(IsEnable() && giftbag.required_level <= Global.character_.level && giftbag.id == m_indexlist[0], __BaseUI._rect(247f, 355f, 226f, 70f), string.Empty))
				{
					if (Global.character_.isGemFull() && (giftbag.id == 2 || giftbag.id == 5))
					{
						__BaseUI.GetMessageBox().setMessage(string.Empty, __BaseUI.load_str(21068u), rmg);
					}
					else if (giftbag.cost > 0)
					{
						DisableAll();
						__BaseUI.GetConsumeUI().setConsume(giftbag.cost, 105, GiftBagOK, new object[1] { giftbag.id });
					}
					else
					{
						cs_gift_bag_get((uint)giftbag.id);
					}
				}
				string text = "gift_bag_item";
				if (!Global.isNewNewbieActivity)
				{
					text = "gift_bag_item_old";
				}
				foreach (object value in Global.getTemplates(text).Values)
				{
					giftbagitem giftbagitem = new giftbagitem();
					if (!Global.isNewNewbieActivity)
					{
						_gift_bag_item_old gift_bag_item_old = value as _gift_bag_item_old;
						giftbagitem = new giftbagitem((int)gift_bag_item_old.id, (int)gift_bag_item_old.idgift_bag, gift_bag_item_old.treasure_type, gift_bag_item_old.param0, gift_bag_item_old.param1);
					}
					else
					{
						_gift_bag_item gift_bag_item = value as _gift_bag_item;
						giftbagitem = new giftbagitem((int)gift_bag_item.id, (int)gift_bag_item.idgift_bag, gift_bag_item.treasure_type, gift_bag_item.param0, gift_bag_item.param1);
					}
					if (giftbagitem.idgift_bag != giftbag.id)
					{
						continue;
					}
					__BaseUI.BeginGroup(__BaseUI._rect(bagtre_pos[num - 1].x, bagtre_pos[num - 1].y + (float)num2 * bagtre_pos[num - 1].z, 225f, 28f));
					__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(0f, 0f, 225f, 28f), ui_fudai_bar3);
					if (giftbagitem.treasure_type == 54 || giftbagitem.treasure_type == 60)
					{
						gb_sptre.make((byte)giftbagitem.treasure_type, giftbagitem.param0, giftbagitem.param1);
						if (gb_sptre.texIcon != null)
						{
							if (gb_sptre.texIcon.name != gb_sptre.getIconName())
							{
								AddAssetSub(gb_sptre.getIconName());
								StartLoadingSub();
							}
							__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(46f, 0f, 30f, 30f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "yindao:" + giftbagitem.treasure_type + "|" + giftbagitem.param0 + "|" + giftbagitem.param1), gb_sptre.texIcon);
							int num3 = 1;
							if (giftbagitem.treasure_type == 60)
							{
								num3 = giftbagitem.param1;
							}
							__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(85f, 7f, 300f, 16f), gb_sptre.strParam[0] + "+" + num3, __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrYellowLight);
						}
						else
						{
							AddAssetSub(gb_sptre.getIconName());
							StartLoadingSub();
						}
					}
					else
					{
						int num4 = 23;
						int num5 = 0;
						switch (giftbagitem.treasure_type)
						{
						case 38:
						case 39:
						case 40:
						case 41:
							num4 = 36;
							num5 = 5;
							break;
						}
						__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(49 - num5, 3 - num5, num4, num4), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "yindao:" + giftbagitem.treasure_type + "|" + giftbagitem.param0 + "|" + giftbagitem.param1), Global.GetIcon(giftbagitem.treasure_type, giftbagitem.param0));
						_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
						treasure.make((byte)giftbagitem.treasure_type, giftbagitem.param0, giftbagitem.param1);
						__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(85f, 7f, 300f, 16f), treasure.strParam[0] + "+" + giftbagitem.param1, __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrYellowLight);
					}
					__BaseUI.EndGroup();
					num2++;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(278f, 254f, 149f, 33f), string.Empty, ui_fdylw);
			}
			giftbag giftbag2 = null;
			if (!Global.isNewNewbieActivity)
			{
				if (Global.getTemplate("gift_bag_old", baglevel + 1) is _gift_bag_old gift_bag_old2)
				{
					giftbag2 = new giftbag((int)gift_bag_old2.id, (int)gift_bag_old2.cost, (int)gift_bag_old2.required_level, (int)gift_bag_old2.required_vip_level);
				}
			}
			else if (Global.getTemplate("gift_bag", baglevel + 1) is _gift_bag gift_bag2)
			{
				giftbag2 = new giftbag((int)gift_bag2.id, (int)gift_bag2.cost, (int)gift_bag2.required_level, (int)gift_bag2.required_vip_level);
			}
			if (giftbag2 == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(511f, 375f, 237f, 71f), ui_fd_chongzhi);
			if (next_level != 0)
			{
				__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(511f, 375f, 237f, 71f), ui_fd_chongzhi);
				__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(595f, 380f, 30f, 21f), ui_vipzi[next_level - 1]);
				if (ui_difudai.name == "ui_fudai1")
				{
					__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(553f, 404f, 56f, 36f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "giftbag" + giftbag2.id), ui_icon_fudai1);
				}
				else
				{
					__BaseUI.Box(__BaseUI._rect(553f, 404f, 56f, 36f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "giftbag" + giftbag2.id), ui_icon_fudai2);
				}
				if (ui_chongzhi3.Button(IsEnable(), __BaseUI._rect(619f, 415f, 80f, 22f), string.Empty))
				{
					RootUI.Coin();
				}
			}
		}
		else
		{
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(278f, 254f, 149f, 33f), string.Empty, ui_fdylw);
		}
	}

	private bool GetAll()
	{
		string text = "gift_bag";
		if (!Global.isNewNewbieActivity)
		{
			text = "gift_bag_old";
		}
		foreach (object value in Global.getTemplates(text).Values)
		{
			giftbag giftbag = new giftbag();
			if (!Global.isNewNewbieActivity)
			{
				_gift_bag_old gift_bag_old = value as _gift_bag_old;
				giftbag = new giftbag((int)gift_bag_old.id, (int)gift_bag_old.cost, (int)gift_bag_old.required_level, (int)gift_bag_old.required_vip_level);
			}
			else
			{
				_gift_bag gift_bag = value as _gift_bag;
				giftbag = new giftbag((int)gift_bag.id, (int)gift_bag.cost, (int)gift_bag.required_level, (int)gift_bag.required_vip_level);
			}
			if (Global.character_.giftbags.ContainsKey((uint)giftbag.id))
			{
				if (!Global.character_.giftbags[(uint)giftbag.id].got && Global.character_.giftbags[(uint)giftbag.id].start_time + 604800 >= TimeUtil.Now)
				{
					return false;
				}
				continue;
			}
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	private bool nowGetall()
	{
		foreach (GiftBag value in Global.character_.giftbags.Values)
		{
			if (!value.got && value.start_time + 604800 >= TimeUtil.Now)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		return true;
	}

	private void GiftBagOK(bool bOK, object[] objs)
	{
		if (bOK)
		{
			int id = (int)objs[0];
			cs_gift_bag_get((uint)id);
		}
		else
		{
			EnableAll();
		}
	}

	public void cs_gift_bag_get(uint id)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSGiftBagGet_0x3B6 cSGiftBagGet_0x3B = new CSGiftBagGet_0x3B6();
			cSGiftBagGet_0x3B.id = id;
			cSGiftBagGet_0x3B.isnew = Global.isNewNewbieActivity;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(950, cSGiftBagGet_0x3B, sc_gift_bag_get);
		}
	}

	public void sc_gift_bag_get(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		sended = false;
		EnableAll();
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		SCGiftBagGet_0x3B6 sCGiftBagGet_0x3B = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCGiftBagGet_0x3B6>(stream);
		if (sCGiftBagGet_0x3B.result != 1)
		{
			return;
		}
		List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
		foreach (Treasure bonu in sCGiftBagGet_0x3B.bonus)
		{
			_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
			treasure.make(bonu);
			list.Add(treasure);
		}
		BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
		battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, EnableAll);
		battleResultUI.MyLoading();
		GiftBag giftBag = new GiftBag();
		giftBag.Set(sCGiftBagGet_0x3B.giftbag);
		if (Global.character_.giftbags.ContainsKey(sCGiftBagGet_0x3B.giftbag.idgift_bag))
		{
			Global.character_.giftbags[giftBag.idgift_bag] = giftBag;
		}
		else
		{
			Global.character_.giftbags.Add(giftBag.idgift_bag, giftBag);
		}
		Global.character_.gold -= (int)sCGiftBagGet_0x3B.goldcost;
		__BaseUI.drawStateBubble("gold-" + sCGiftBagGet_0x3B.goldcost);
		m_indexlist.Clear();
		foreach (GiftBag value in Global.character_.giftbags.Values)
		{
			if (!value.got && value.start_time + 604800 > TimeUtil.Now)
			{
				m_indexlist.Add(value.idgift_bag);
			}
		}
		SortIdGift();
		nowpage = 1;
	}

	public void cs_cost_times_award_get(uint cost_type)
	{
		if (!sended)
		{
			sended = true;
			CSCostTimesAwardGet_0x3C4 cSCostTimesAwardGet_0x3C = new CSCostTimesAwardGet_0x3C4();
			cSCostTimesAwardGet_0x3C.cost_type = cost_type;
			RootUI.m_client.Send(964, cSCostTimesAwardGet_0x3C, sc_cost_times_award_get);
		}
	}

	public void sc_cost_times_award_get(MemoryStream stream, object arg)
	{
		sended = false;
		EnableAll();
		if (stream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		SCCostTimesAwardGet_0x3C4 sCCostTimesAwardGet_0x3C = JSProtoBuf.Serializer.Deserialize<SCCostTimesAwardGet_0x3C4>(stream);
		if (sCCostTimesAwardGet_0x3C.result != 1)
		{
			return;
		}
		List<_Treasure> list = new List<_Treasure>();
		foreach (Treasure bonuse in sCCostTimesAwardGet_0x3C.bonuses)
		{
			_Treasure treasure = new _Treasure();
			treasure.make(bonuse);
			list.Add(treasure);
		}
		BattleResultUI battleResultUI = __BaseUI.GetBattleResultUI();
		battleResultUI.Set(rmg, string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, list, EnableAll);
		battleResultUI.MyLoading();
		foreach (db_character_cost_times award in sCCostTimesAwardGet_0x3C.awards)
		{
			CostTimesAward costTimesAward = new CostTimesAward();
			costTimesAward.Set(award);
			Global.character_.costtimesawards.Add(costTimesAward.idcost_times, costTimesAward);
		}
	}

	private bool checkCostTimes(uint cost_type)
	{
		if (Global.costtimeswave.start_time <= TimeUtil.Now && Global.costtimeswave.end_time >= TimeUtil.Now)
		{
			foreach (Database.CostTimesTemplate template in Global.costtimeswave.templates)
			{
				if (template.cost_times_type == cost_type)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public bool checkCostTimes1()
	{
		return checkCostTimes(1u);
	}

	public bool checkCostTimes2()
	{
		return checkCostTimes(2u);
	}

	public bool checkCostTimes3()
	{
		return checkCostTimes(3u);
	}

	public bool checkCostTimes4()
	{
		return checkCostTimes(4u);
	}

	public bool checkCostTimes5()
	{
		return checkCostTimes(5u);
	}

	private void DrawCostTimes(uint cost_type)
	{
		List<Database.CostTimesTemplate> list = new List<Database.CostTimesTemplate>();
		foreach (Database.CostTimesTemplate template in Global.costtimeswave.templates)
		{
			if (template.cost_times_type == cost_type)
			{
				list.Add(template);
			}
		}
		list.Sort((Database.CostTimesTemplate x, Database.CostTimesTemplate y) => x.times.CompareTo(y.times));
		int num = 0;
		int num2 = 0;
		int num3 = 0;
		int num4 = 0;
		Vector2 vector = default(Vector2);
		switch (cost_type)
		{
		case 1u:
		case 2u:
		case 5u:
			num = 259;
			num2 = 162;
			num3 = 425;
			num4 = 409;
			vector = new Vector2(426f, 123f);
			break;
		case 3u:
		case 4u:
			num = 295;
			num2 = 149;
			num3 = 462;
			num4 = 402;
			vector = new Vector2(464f, 106f);
			break;
		}
		int num5 = 39;
		int num6 = 234;
		int num7 = Math.Max(list.Count * num5, num6);
		uint num8 = 0u;
		if (Global.character_.costtimesrecords.ContainsKey(cost_type))
		{
			num8 = Global.character_.costtimesrecords[cost_type].times;
		}
		bool flag = false;
		Vector2 scrollpos = ScrollControl.infos[costtimes_scrollid[cost_type - 1]].scrollpos;
		scrollpos = GUIHacks.BeginScrollView(__BaseUI._rect(num, num2, 442f, num6), scrollpos, __BaseUI._rect(0f, 0f, 417f, num7), useHorizontal: false, num7 > num6, new GUIStyle(), __BaseUI._vertScroll1[0], 39, 0);
		ScrollControl.infos[costtimes_scrollid[cost_type - 1]].scrollpos = scrollpos;
		int num9 = 0;
		foreach (Database.CostTimesTemplate item in list)
		{
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(0f, num9 * num5, 417f, 36f), string.Empty, ui_fb_bar[cost_type - 1]);
			bool flag2 = false;
			if (num8 >= item.times)
			{
				flag2 = true;
			}
			bool flag3 = false;
			if (Global.character_.costtimesawards.ContainsKey(item.idcost_times))
			{
				flag3 = true;
			}
			Color clr = __BaseUI._clrGreen;
			if (!flag2)
			{
				clr = __BaseUI._clrRed;
			}
			GUIStyle style = ui_fb_weidacheng;
			if (flag3)
			{
				style = ui_fb_yilingqu;
			}
			else if (flag2)
			{
				style = ui_fb_dacheng;
				if (!flag)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			Vector2 vector2 = default(Vector2);
			Vector2 vector3 = default(Vector2);
			Vector2 vector4 = default(Vector2);
			switch (cost_type)
			{
			case 1u:
				vector2 = new Vector2(337f, 168f);
				vector3 = new Vector2(399f, 168f);
				vector4 = new Vector2(525f, 160f);
				break;
			case 2u:
				vector2 = new Vector2(387f, 168f);
				vector3 = new Vector2(446f, 168f);
				vector4 = new Vector2(525f, 160f);
				break;
			case 3u:
				vector2 = new Vector2(423f, 155f);
				vector3 = new Vector2(480f, 155f);
				vector4 = new Vector2(576f, 147f);
				break;
			case 4u:
				vector2 = new Vector2(419f, 155f);
				vector3 = new Vector2(478f, 155f);
				vector4 = new Vector2(576f, 147f);
				break;
			case 5u:
				vector2 = new Vector2(387f, 168f);
				vector3 = new Vector2(446f, 168f);
				vector4 = new Vector2(525f, 160f);
				break;
			}
			foreach (_Treasure bonu in item.bonus)
			{
				if (bonu.texIcon == null)
				{
					AddAssetSub(bonu.getIconName());
					StartLoadingSub();
				}
			}
			__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(vector2.x - (float)num, vector2.y - (float)num2 + (float)(num9 * num5), 31f, 21f), item.times.ToString(), __BaseUI._numCenter, clr);
			__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(vector3.x - (float)num, vector3.y - (float)num2 + (float)(num9 * num5), 49f, 21f), "(" + Math.Min(num8, item.times) + "/" + item.times + ")", __BaseUI._numCenter, clr);
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(vector4.x - (float)num, vector4.y - (float)num2 + (float)(num9 * num5), 113f, 39f), new GUIContent(string.Empty, "cost_times" + item.idcost_times), style, __BaseUI._clrBlack);
			num9++;
		}
		for (; num9 < 6; num9++)
		{
			__BaseUI.Label(__BaseUI._rect(0f, num9 * num5, 417f, 36f), string.Empty, ui_fb_bar[5]);
		}
		GUIHacks.EndScrollView();
		if (button_lingqu2.Button(flag, __BaseUI._rect(num3, num4, 98f, 30f), string.Empty))
		{
			cs_cost_times_award_get(cost_type);
		}
		uint start_time = Global.costtimeswave.start_time;
		uint end_time = Global.costtimeswave.end_time;
		DateTime dateTime = new DateTime(1970, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc).AddSeconds(start_time).ToLocalTime();
		DateTime dateTime2 = new DateTime(1970, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc).AddSeconds(end_time).ToLocalTime();
		__BaseUI.OutlinedLabel(__BaseUI._rect(vector.x, vector.y, 300f, 16f), "(" + dateTime.Month + "/" + dateTime.Day + " " + dateTime.Hour + ":00-" + dateTime2.Month + "/" + dateTime2.Day + " " + dateTime2.Hour + ":00)", __BaseUI._textNLeft, __BaseUI._clrWhite);
	}

	private void loadCostTimesTexture(AssetBundle uibundle)
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < ui_fb_bar.Length; i++)
		{
			ui_fb_bar[i] = new GUIStyle();
			ui_fb_bar[i].normal.background = uibundle.Load("ui_fb_bar" + (i + 1)) as Texture2D;
		}
		button_lingqu2.make(uibundle, "button_lingqu2");
		ui_fb_dacheng.normal.background = uibundle.Load("ui_fb_dacheng") as Texture2D;
		ui_fb_weidacheng.normal.background = uibundle.Load("ui_fb_weidacheng") as Texture2D;
		ui_fb_yilingqu.normal.background = uibundle.Load("ui_fb_yilingqu") as Texture2D;
	}

	private bool checkCostTimeAward(uint cost_type)
	{
		uint num = 0u;
		if (Global.character_.costtimesrecords.ContainsKey(cost_type))
		{
			num = Global.character_.costtimesrecords[cost_type].times;
		}
		foreach (Database.CostTimesTemplate template in Global.costtimeswave.templates)
		{
			if (template.cost_times_type == cost_type)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				if (num >= template.times)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				bool flag2 = false;
				if (Global.character_.costtimesawards.ContainsKey(template.idcost_times))
				{
					flag2 = true;
				}
				if (!flag2 && flag)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public bool checkCostTimesAward1()
	{
		return checkCostTimeAward(1u);
	}

	public bool checkCostTimesAward2()
	{
		return checkCostTimeAward(2u);
	}

	public bool checkCostTimesAward3()
	{
		return checkCostTimeAward(3u);
	}

	public bool checkCostTimesAward4()
	{
		return checkCostTimeAward(4u);
	}

	public bool checkCostTimesAward5()
	{
		return checkCostTimeAward(5u);
	}
